`cms/tests/Feature/Content/BlockNormalizerTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Content;

use App\Content\Blocks\BlockNormalizer;
use App\Models\HtmlPage;
use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Tests\TestCase;

class BlockNormalizerTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private function media(string $kind): MediaAsset
    {
        return MediaAsset::create(['kind' => $kind, 'token' => Str::random(32), 'original_name' => "Grade V Syllabus.{$kind}", 'mime' => 'x', 'bytes' => 10]);
    }

    private function normalize(array $blocks, bool $super = false, array $previous = []): array
    {
        return app(BlockNormalizer::class)->normalize($blocks, $super, $previous);
    }

    private function assertBlockError(array $blocks, string $key, bool $super = false, array $previous = []): void
    {
        try {
            $this->normalize($blocks, $super, $previous);
            $this->fail('Expected ValidationException');
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey($key, $e->errors());
        }
    }

    public function test_normalizes_every_block_type(): void
    {
        $img = $this->media('image');
        $pdf = $this->media('pdf');
        $vid = $this->media('video');

        $out = $this->normalize([
            ['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p>Hello<script>x</script></p>'],
            ['type' => 'image', 'media_id' => $img->id, 'caption' => ' Assembly '],
            ['type' => 'gallery', 'items' => [['media_id' => $img->id], ['media_id' => $img->id, 'caption' => 'b']]],
            ['type' => 'pdf', 'files' => [['media_id' => $pdf->id, 'label' => ''], ['media_id' => $pdf->id, 'label' => 'French 2L']]],
            ['type' => 'link', 'label' => 'Register', 'url' => 'https://forms.example.com/x'],
            ['type' => 'youtube', 'url' => 'https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ', 'mode' => 'player'],
            ['type' => 'video', 'media_id' => $vid->id],
            ['type' => 'table', 'has_header' => true, 'paste' => "A\tB\n1\t2"],
        ]);

        $this->assertSame(['type' => 'text', 'version' => 1, 'html' => '<p>Hello</p>'], $out[0]);
        $this->assertSame('Assembly', $out[1]['caption']);
        $this->assertNull($out[2]['items'][0]['caption']);
        $this->assertSame('Grade V Syllabus', $out[3]['files'][0]['label']);
        $this->assertSame('dQw4w9WgXcQ', $out[5]['video_id']);
        $this->assertSame([['A', 'B'], ['1', '2']], $out[7]['rows']);
        $this->assertTrue($out[7]['has_header']);
        foreach ($out as $block) {
            $this->assertSame(1, $block['version']);
        }
    }

    public function test_unknown_type(): void
    {
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'marquee']], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_empty_text(): void
    {
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p> <script>x</script></p>']], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_media_must_exist_and_match_kind(): void
    {
        $pdf = $this->media('pdf');
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'image', 'media_id' => $pdf->id]], 'blocks.0');
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'image', 'media_id' => 999999]], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_gallery_limit(): void
    {
        config(['cms.limits.gallery_images' => 2]);
        $img = $this->media('image');
        $items = array_fill(0, 3, ['media_id' => $img->id]);
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'gallery', 'items' => $items]], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_link_rejects_javascript_urls(): void
    {
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'link', 'label' => 'x', 'url' => 'javascript:alert(1)']], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_youtube_rejects_other_sites(): void
    {
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'youtube', 'url' => 'https://vimeo.com/1', 'mode' => 'link']], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_editor_cannot_add_html_page_block(): void
    {
        $page = HtmlPage::create(['token' => Str::random(32), 'title' => 'Seating', 'bytes' => 10]);
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'html_page', 'html_page_id' => $page->id, 'mode' => 'embed']], 'blocks.0');
    }

    public function test_editor_can_keep_or_move_existing_html_page_block(): void
    {
        $page = HtmlPage::create(['token' => Str::random(32), 'title' => 'Seating', 'bytes' => 10]);
        $existing = ['type' => 'html_page', 'version' => 1, 'html_page_id' => $page->id, 'mode' => 'embed'];
        $text = ['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p>Intro</p>'];

        $out = $this->normalize([$text, $existing], false, [$existing]);
        $this->assertSame('html_page', $out[1]['type']);

        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'html_page', 'html_page_id' => $page->id, 'mode' => 'link']], 'blocks.0', false, [$existing]);
    }

    public function test_super_admin_can_add_html_page_block(): void
    {
        $page = HtmlPage::create(['token' => Str::random(32), 'title' => 'Seating', 'bytes' => 10]);
        $out = $this->normalize([['type' => 'html_page', 'html_page_id' => $page->id, 'mode' => 'link']], true);
        $this->assertSame($page->id, $out[0]['html_page_id']);
    }

    public function test_table_limits_become_block_errors(): void
    {
        config(['cms.limits.table_rows' => 1]);
        $this->assertBlockError([['type' => 'table', 'paste' => "a\nb"]], 'blocks.0');
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Content/Blocks/TextSanitizer.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content\Blocks;

use Symfony\Component\HtmlSanitizer\HtmlSanitizer;
use Symfony\Component\HtmlSanitizer\HtmlSanitizerConfig;

class TextSanitizer
{
    private HtmlSanitizer $sanitizer;

    public function __construct()
    {
        $config = (new HtmlSanitizerConfig())
            ->allowElement('p')
            ->allowElement('br')
            ->allowElement('strong')
            ->allowElement('b')
            ->allowElement('em')
            ->allowElement('i')
            ->allowElement('ul')
            ->allowElement('ol')
            ->allowElement('li')
            ->allowElement('a', ['href'])
            ->allowLinkSchemes(['http', 'https', 'mailto', 'tel'])
            ->allowRelativeLinks(false)
            ->forceAttribute('a', 'rel', 'noopener noreferrer')
            ->withMaxInputLength(200000);

        $this->sanitizer = new HtmlSanitizer($config);
    }

    public function clean(string $html): string
    {
        return trim($this->sanitizer->sanitize($html));
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Content/Blocks/TableParser.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content\Blocks;

use InvalidArgumentException;

final class TableParser
{
    /** @return array<int, array<int, string>> */
    public static function parse(string $pasted): array
    {
        $pasted = str_replace(["\r\n", "\r"], "\n", $pasted);
        $firstLine = strtok($pasted, "\n") ?: '';
        $delimiter = str_contains($firstLine, "\t") ? "\t" : ',';

        $handle = fopen('php://temp', 'r+');
        fwrite($handle, $pasted);
        rewind($handle);

        $rows = [];
        while (($row = fgetcsv($handle, 0, $delimiter, '"', '')) !== false) {
            $rows[] = array_map(fn ($cell) => mb_substr(trim((string) $cell), 0, 500), $row);
        }
        fclose($handle);

        return self::fromRows($rows);
    }

    /** @return array<int, array<int, string>> */
    public static function fromRows(array $rows): array
    {
        $rows = array_values(array_map(
            fn ($row) => array_map(fn ($cell) => mb_substr(trim((string) $cell), 0, 500), array_values((array) $row)),
            $rows
        ));

        while ($rows !== [] && implode('', end($rows)) === '') {
            array_pop($rows);
        }
        if ($rows === []) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('The table is empty. Copy cells from Excel and paste them here.');
        }

        $maxRows = config('cms.limits.table_rows');
        $maxCols = config('cms.limits.table_cols');
        $width = max(array_map('count', $rows));

        if (count($rows) > $maxRows) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException("The table has ".count($rows)." rows. The limit is {$maxRows}. Split it into two tables or upload a PDF.");
        }
        if ($width > $maxCols) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException("The table has {$width} columns. The limit is {$maxCols}. Upload it as a PDF instead.");
        }

        return array_map(fn ($row) => array_pad($row, $width, ''), $rows);
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Content/Blocks/YouTube.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content\Blocks;

final class YouTube
{
    private const HOSTS = ['youtube.com', 'www.youtube.com', 'm.youtube.com', 'youtu.be', 'www.youtube-nocookie.com'];

    public static function videoId(string $url): ?string
    {
        $parts = parse_url(trim($url));
        if (! is_array($parts) || ! in_array(strtolower($parts['host'] ?? ''), self::HOSTS, true)) {
            return null;
        }
        if (! in_array(strtolower($parts['scheme'] ?? ''), ['http', 'https'], true)) {
            return null;
        }

        $host = strtolower($parts['host']);
        $path = $parts['path'] ?? '';
        $candidate = null;

        if ($host === 'youtu.be') {
            $candidate = ltrim($path, '/');
        } elseif (preg_match('#^/(shorts|embed|live)/([^/?]+)#', $path, $m)) {
            $candidate = $m[2];
        } elseif ($path === '/watch') {
            parse_str($parts['query'] ?? '', $query);
            $candidate = $query['v'] ?? null;
        }

        return is_string($candidate) && preg_match('/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/', $candidate) ? $candidate : null;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: BlockNormalizer**

`cms/app/Content/Blocks/BlockNormalizer.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content\Blocks;

use App\Models\HtmlPage;
use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use InvalidArgumentException;

class BlockNormalizer
{
    public const MAX_BLOCKS = 100;

    public function __construct(private readonly TextSanitizer $sanitizer) {}

    public function normalize(array $input, bool $isSuperAdmin, array $previous = []): array
    {
        if (count($input) > self::MAX_BLOCKS) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['blocks' => 'A post can have at most '.self::MAX_BLOCKS.' blocks.']);
        }

        $errors = [];
        $out = [];

        foreach (array_values($input) as $i => $block) {
            try {
                $out[] = $this->one(is_array($block) ? $block : [], $isSuperAdmin, $previous);
            } catch (InvalidArgumentException $e) {
                $errors["blocks.{$i}"] = 'Block '.($i + 1).': '.$e->getMessage();
            }
        }

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages($errors);
        }

        return $out;
    }

    private function one(array $b, bool $isSuperAdmin, array $previous): array
    {
        $type = $b['type'] ?? null;

        return match ($type) {
            'text' => $this->text($b),
            'image' => ['type' => 'image', 'version' => 1, 'media_id' => $this->mediaId($b['media_id'] ?? null, 'image'), 'caption' => $this->caption($b['caption'] ?? null)],
            'gallery' => $this->gallery($b),
            'pdf' => $this->pdf($b),
            'link' => $this->link($b),
            'youtube' => $this->youtube($b),
            'video' => ['type' => 'video', 'version' => 1, 'media_id' => $this->mediaId($b['media_id'] ?? null, 'video'), 'caption' => $this->caption($b['caption'] ?? null)],
            'table' => $this->table($b),
            'html_page' => $this->htmlPage($b, $isSuperAdmin, $previous),
            default => throw new InvalidArgumentException('Unknown block type.'),
        };
    }

    private function text(array $b): array
    {
        $html = $this->sanitizer->clean((string) ($b['html'] ?? ''));
        if (trim(html_entity_decode(strip_tags($html))) === '') {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('This text block is empty. Type something or remove the block.');
        }

        return ['type' => 'text', 'version' => 1, 'html' => $html];
    }

    private function mediaId(mixed $id, string $kind): int
    {
        $asset = is_numeric($id) ? MediaAsset::find((int) $id) : null;
        if ($asset === null || $asset->kind !== $kind) {
            $what = ['image' => 'photo', 'pdf' => 'PDF', 'video' => 'video'][$kind];
            throw new InvalidArgumentException("The {$what} is missing. Upload it again.");
        }

        return $asset->id;
    }

    private function caption(mixed $caption): ?string
    {
        $caption = trim((string) $caption);
        if (mb_strlen($caption) > 300) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Captions can be at most 300 characters.');
        }

        return $caption === '' ? null : $caption;
    }

    private function gallery(array $b): array
    {
        $items = array_values((array) ($b['items'] ?? []));
        $max = config('cms.limits.gallery_images');
        if ($items === []) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Add at least one photo to the gallery.');
        }
        if (count($items) > $max) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('A gallery can have at most '.$max.' photos. Split it into two galleries.');
        }

        return ['type' => 'gallery', 'version' => 1, 'items' => array_map(fn ($item) => [
            'media_id' => $this->mediaId($item['media_id'] ?? null, 'image'),
            'caption' => $this->caption($item['caption'] ?? null),
        ], $items)];
    }

    private function pdf(array $b): array
    {
        $files = array_values((array) ($b['files'] ?? []));
        if ($files === [] || count($files) > 20) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Add between 1 and 20 PDFs.');
        }

        return ['type' => 'pdf', 'version' => 1, 'files' => array_map(function ($file) {
            $id = $this->mediaId($file['media_id'] ?? null, 'pdf');
            $label = trim((string) ($file['label'] ?? ''));
            if ($label === '') {
                $label = pathinfo(MediaAsset::find($id)->original_name, PATHINFO_FILENAME);
            }

            return ['media_id' => $id, 'label' => mb_substr($label, 0, 150)];
        }, $files)];
    }

    private function link(array $b): array
    {
        $label = trim((string) ($b['label'] ?? ''));
        $url = trim((string) ($b['url'] ?? ''));
        $scheme = strtolower((string) parse_url($url, PHP_URL_SCHEME));
        if ($label === '' || mb_strlen($label) > 100) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Give the button a label (up to 100 characters).');
        }
        if (! in_array($scheme, ['http', 'https'], true) || filter_var($url, FILTER_VALIDATE_URL) === false) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('The link must be a full web address starting with https://');
        }

        return ['type' => 'link', 'version' => 1, 'label' => $label, 'url' => $url];
    }

    private function youtube(array $b): array
    {
        $url = trim((string) ($b['url'] ?? ''));
        $id = YouTube::videoId($url);
        if ($id === null) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('That is not a YouTube video link. Copy the link from the YouTube Share button.');
        }
        $mode = ($b['mode'] ?? 'link') === 'player' ? 'player' : 'link';

        return ['type' => 'youtube', 'version' => 1, 'url' => $url, 'video_id' => $id, 'mode' => $mode];
    }

    private function table(array $b): array
    {
        $rows = isset($b['paste']) && is_string($b['paste'])
            ? TableParser::parse($b['paste'])
            : TableParser::fromRows((array) ($b['rows'] ?? []));

        return ['type' => 'table', 'version' => 1, 'has_header' => (bool) ($b['has_header'] ?? false), 'rows' => $rows];
    }

    private function htmlPage(array $b, bool $isSuperAdmin, array $previous): array
    {
        $normalized = [
            'type' => 'html_page',
            'version' => 1,
            'html_page_id' => (int) ($b['html_page_id'] ?? 0),
            'mode' => ($b['mode'] ?? 'link') === 'embed' ? 'embed' : 'link',
        ];

        if (! HtmlPage::whereKey($normalized['html_page_id'])->exists()) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('That custom page no longer exists.');
        }

        if (! $isSuperAdmin) {
            $unchanged = collect($previous)->contains(fn ($p) => ($p['type'] ?? null) === 'html_page'
                && (int) ($p['html_page_id'] ?? 0) === $normalized['html_page_id']
                && ($p['mode'] ?? null) === $normalized['mode']);
            if (! $unchanged) {
                throw new InvalidArgumentException('Only IT (Super admin) can add or change custom page blocks.');
            }
        }

        return $normalized;
    }
}
```

Re-saving a table sends `rows` back, which goes through `TableParser::fromRows` without any text round-trip, so multi-line cells are preserved.

- [ ] **Step 6: SearchText**

`cms/app/Content/SearchText.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content;

use App\Models\Post;

final class SearchText
{
    public static function fromPost(Post $post): string
    {
        $parts = [$post->title, $post->summary, $post->newsletter_term];

        foreach ($post->blocks ?? [] as $block) {
            $parts = array_merge($parts, match ($block['type'] ?? null) {
                'text' => [html_entity_decode(strip_tags(str_replace(['</p>', '<br>', '</li>'], ' ', $block['html'] ?? '')))],
                'image', 'video' => [$block['caption'] ?? null],
                'gallery' => array_column($block['items'] ?? [], 'caption'),
                'pdf' => array_column($block['files'] ?? [], 'label'),
                'link' => [$block['label'] ?? null],
                'table' => array_merge(...array_map(fn ($r) => array_values($r), $block['rows'] ?? [[]])),
                default => [],
            });
        }

        return trim(preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', implode(' ', array_filter($parts, fn ($p) => is_string($p) && $p !== ''))));
    }
}
```

### Task 8: PostService (drafts, publish, schedule, edit, small fix, pin, delete, restore, purge, versions)

**Decision refinement (record in spec §7 item 4 during this task):** Autosave every 10 seconds goes to the **browser's localStorage** on that device and is not a server save. The server write happens only on explicit **Save draft** or **Publish**, both of which need the typed name and are audited. This avoids flooding the audit log and conflicts with the "name starts blank every time" rule. Shared drafts still work, because "Save draft" stores them on the server.

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/app/Content/PublishOptions.php`, `cms/app/Content/PostService.php`, `cms/app/Content/PostRules.php`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-cms-design.md` (§7 item 4 wording)
- Test: `cms/tests/Feature/Content/PostServiceTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Actor`, `AuditLogger::record` (Task 3); `GradeScope`, `AcademicYear` (Task 4); `Post`, `PostVersion`, `PostStatus`, `Section` (Task 5); `MediaAsset` (Task 6); `BlockNormalizer::normalize`, `SearchText::fromPost` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `PublishOptions` — `public function __construct(public readonly ?CarbonImmutable $publishAt = null, public readonly ?CarbonImmutable $removeOn = null, public readonly ?CarbonImmutable $pinnedUntil = null, public readonly bool $smallFix = false)`.
  - `PostService`:
    - `saveDraft(?Post $post, Section $section, array $data, Actor $actor, bool $isSuperAdmin): Post`
    - `publish(Post $post, array $data, PublishOptions $options, Actor $actor, bool $isSuperAdmin): Post` (also used for edits of live posts)
    - `unschedule(Post $post, Actor $actor): Post`
    - `setPin(Post $post, ?CarbonImmutable $until, Actor $actor): Post`
    - `delete(Post $post, Actor $actor): void`
    - `restore(Post $post, Actor $actor): Post`
    - `restoreVersion(Post $post, PostVersion $version, Actor $actor): Post`
    - `purgeExpired(?CarbonInterface $now = null): int`
  - `PostRules::assertPublishable(Post $post): void` (throws `ValidationException`).
- `$data` keys (all optional unless noted): `title` (required), `summary`, `blocks` (array), `grades` (array for `GradeScope::fromInput`), `event_date_start`, `event_date_end` (Y-m-d), `cover_media_id`, `newsletter_series_id`, `newsletter_term`, `newsletter_academic_year` ("2026-27"), `newsletter_pdf_media_id`, `newsletter_link`.

`cms/tests/Feature/Content/PostServiceTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Content;

use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Content\PostStatus;
use App\Content\PublishOptions;
use App\Content\Section;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use App\Models\NewsletterSeries;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PostServiceTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private PostService $service;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->travelTo(CarbonImmutable::parse('2026-09-15 10:00:00'));
        $this->service = app(PostService::class);
    }

    private function actor(string $name = 'Ms. Priya'): Actor
    {
        return new Actor($name, 1, 'editor', '10.0.0.1', 'UA', 'sess');
    }

    private function data(array $overrides = []): array
    {
        return array_merge([
            'title' => 'Grade V Half Yearly Syllabus',
            'summary' => null,
            'blocks' => [['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p>Please find the syllabus.</p>']],
            'grades' => ['mode' => 'none'],
        ], $overrides);
    }

    private function media(string $kind): MediaAsset
    {
        return MediaAsset::create(['kind' => $kind, 'token' => Str::random(32), 'original_name' => "f.{$kind}", 'mime' => 'x', 'bytes' => 1]);
    }

    public function test_save_draft_creates_version_and_audit(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Circular, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);

        $this->assertSame(PostStatus::Draft, $post->status);
        $this->assertNull($post->slug);
        $this->assertSame(1, $post->versions()->count());
        $entry = AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.draft_saved')->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('Ms. Priya', $entry->actor_name);
        $this->assertSame($post->id, (int) $entry->target_id);
    }

    public function test_publish_now_sets_dates_slug_year_and_search_text(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Circular, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);
        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor('Mr. Khan'), false);

        $this->assertSame(PostStatus::Published, $post->status);
        $this->assertSame('2026-09-15 10:00:00', $post->first_published_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
        $this->assertEquals($post->first_published_at, $post->sort_at);
        $this->assertNull($post->updated_at_public);
        $this->assertSame('grade-v-half-yearly-syllabus', $post->slug);
        $this->assertSame('2026-27', $post->academic_year);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Please find the syllabus', $post->search_text);
        $this->assertSame('live', $post->adminStatus());
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.published')->where('actor_name', 'Mr. Khan')->exists());
    }

    public function test_schedule_then_unschedule(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Info, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);
        $at = CarbonImmutable::parse('2026-09-20 07:00:00');

        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(publishAt: $at), $this->actor(), false);
        $this->assertSame('scheduled', $post->adminStatus());
        $this->assertEquals($at, $post->sort_at);

        $post = $this->service->unschedule($post, $this->actor());
        $this->assertSame('draft', $post->adminStatus());
        $this->assertNull($post->publish_at);
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.unscheduled')->exists());
    }

    public function test_schedule_in_past_is_rejected(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Info, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);
        $this->expectException(ValidationException::class);
        $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(publishAt: now()->subMinute()->toImmutable()), $this->actor(), false);
    }

    public function test_editing_live_post_bumps_to_top_and_marks_updated(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->section(Section::Circular)->published(now()->subDays(5))->create(['slug' => 'orig']);
        $this->travel(1)->hours();

        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['title' => 'Revised syllabus']), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);

        $this->assertSame('2026-09-15 11:00:00', $post->sort_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
        $this->assertSame('2026-09-15 11:00:00', $post->updated_at_public->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
        $this->assertSame('2026-09-10', $post->first_published_at->format('Y-m-d'));
        $this->assertSame('orig', $post->slug, 'slug never changes after first publish');
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.edited')->exists());
    }

    public function test_small_fix_keeps_position_but_marks_updated(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published(now()->subDays(5))->create();
        $sortBefore = $post->sort_at;

        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(smallFix: true), $this->actor(), false);

        $this->assertEquals($sortBefore, $post->sort_at);
        $this->assertNotNull($post->updated_at_public);
    }

    public function test_live_post_cannot_be_rescheduled(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();
        $this->expectException(ValidationException::class);
        $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(publishAt: now()->addDay()->toImmutable()), $this->actor(), false);
    }

    public function test_pin_requires_future_date_and_remove_on_must_be_future(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();

        try {
            $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(pinnedUntil: now()->subDay()->toImmutable()), $this->actor(), false);
            $this->fail('pin in past accepted');
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey('pinned_until', $e->errors());
        }

        try {
            $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(removeOn: now()->subDay()->toImmutable()), $this->actor(), false);
            $this->fail('remove_on in past accepted');
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey('remove_on', $e->errors());
        }

        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(pinnedUntil: now()->addWeek()->toImmutable()), $this->actor(), false);
        $this->assertTrue($post->isPinnedAt(now()));
    }

    public function test_set_pin_and_unpin(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();
        $post = $this->service->setPin($post, now()->addDays(3)->toImmutable(), $this->actor());
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.pinned')->exists());
        $post = $this->service->setPin($post, null, $this->actor());
        $this->assertNull($post->pinned_until);
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.unpinned')->exists());
    }

    public function test_event_requires_cover_and_date_to_publish(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Event, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);
        try {
            $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
            $this->fail('event without cover published');
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey('cover_media_id', $e->errors());
            $this->assertArrayHasKey('event_date_start', $e->errors());
        }

        $cover = $this->media('image');
        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['cover_media_id' => $cover->id, 'event_date_start' => '2026-09-10']), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
        $this->assertSame('live', $post->adminStatus());
    }

    public function test_newsletter_requires_series_year_term_cover_and_exactly_one_of_pdf_or_link(): void
    {
        $series = NewsletterSeries::create(['name' => 'Kindergarten']);
        $cover = $this->media('image');
        $pdf = $this->media('pdf');
        $base = $this->data([
            'blocks' => [],
            'newsletter_series_id' => $series->id,
            'newsletter_academic_year' => '2026-27',
            'newsletter_term' => 'Term I',
            'cover_media_id' => $cover->id,
        ]);
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Newsletter, $base, $this->actor(), false);

        try {
            $this->service->publish($post, $base + ['newsletter_pdf_media_id' => $pdf->id, 'newsletter_link' => 'https://x.test/n'], new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
            $this->fail('both pdf and link accepted');
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey('newsletter_pdf_media_id', $e->errors());
        }

        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $base + ['newsletter_pdf_media_id' => $pdf->id], new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
        $this->assertSame('2026-27', $post->academic_year);
    }

    public function test_editor_block_rules_apply_on_publish(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Info, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);
        $this->expectException(ValidationException::class);
        $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['blocks' => [['type' => 'link', 'label' => 'x', 'url' => 'javascript:1']]]), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
    }

    public function test_delete_restore_and_purge_after_seven_days(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create(['title' => 'To delete']);
        $this->service->delete($post, $this->actor());
        $this->assertSame('deleted', Post::withTrashed()->find($post->id)->adminStatus());

        $restored = $this->service->restore(Post::withTrashed()->find($post->id), $this->actor());
        $this->assertSame('live', $restored->adminStatus());

        $this->service->delete($restored, $this->actor());
        $this->travel(6)->days();
        $this->assertSame(0, $this->service->purgeExpired());
        $this->travel(1)->days();
        $this->travel(1)->minutes();
        $this->assertSame(1, $this->service->purgeExpired());

        $tomb = Post::withTrashed()->find($post->id);
        $this->assertNotNull($tomb->purged_at);
        $this->assertSame([], $tomb->blocks);
        $this->assertSame(0, $tomb->versions()->count());
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.purged')->where('actor_name', 'System (scheduled)')->exists());
        $this->assertSame(['post.deleted', 'post.restored', 'post.deleted', 'post.purged'], AuditEntry::orderBy('id')->pluck('action')->all());
    }

    public function test_every_save_writes_incrementing_versions_and_restore_version(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Info, $this->data(['title' => 'First']), $this->actor(), false);
        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['title' => 'Second']), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
        $post = $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['title' => 'Third']), new PublishOptions(smallFix: true), $this->actor(), false);

        $this->assertSame([3, 2, 1], $post->versions()->pluck('version')->all());

        $v1 = $post->versions()->where('version', 1)->first();
        $post = $this->service->restoreVersion($post, $v1, $this->actor('IT'));

        $this->assertSame('First', $post->title);
        $this->assertSame('live', $post->adminStatus(), 'restore keeps the post live');
        $this->assertSame(4, $post->versions()->max('version'));
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.version_restored')->where('actor_name', 'IT')->exists());
    }

    public function test_rejected_publish_leaves_model_unchanged(): void
    {
        $post = $this->service->saveDraft(null, Section::Event, $this->data(), $this->actor(), false);
        try {
            $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['title' => 'Changed']), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);
        } catch (ValidationException) {
        }
        $this->assertSame('draft', $post->adminStatus());
        $this->assertSame('Grade V Half Yearly Syllabus', $post->title);
        $this->assertFalse(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.published')->exists());
    }

    public function test_audit_entries_include_before_and_after(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create(['title' => 'Before title']);
        $this->service->publish($post, $this->data(['title' => 'After title']), new PublishOptions(), $this->actor(), false);

        $entry = AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.edited')->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('Before title', $entry->beforeData()['title']);
        $this->assertSame('After title', $entry->afterData()['title']);
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content;

use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use App\Models\NewsletterSeries;
use App\Models\Post;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

final class PostRules
{
    public static function assertPublishable(Post $post): void
    {
        $errors = [];

        if (trim((string) $post->title) === '') {
            $errors['title'] = 'Add a title.';
        }

        if ($post->section === Section::Event || $post->section === Section::Newsletter) {
            if (! MediaAsset::whereKey($post->cover_media_id)->where('kind', 'image')->exists()) {
                $errors['cover_media_id'] = 'Add a cover photo.';
            }
        }

        if ($post->section === Section::Event && $post->event_date_start === null) {
            $errors['event_date_start'] = 'Add the date the event happened.';
        }

        if ($post->section === Section::Newsletter) {
            if (! NewsletterSeries::whereKey($post->newsletter_series_id)->exists()) {
                $errors['newsletter_series_id'] = 'Choose which newsletter this is.';
            }
            if (! preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/', (string) $post->academic_year)) {
                $errors['newsletter_academic_year'] = 'Choose the academic year.';
            }
            if (trim((string) $post->newsletter_term) === '') {
                $errors['newsletter_term'] = 'Add the term, for example "Term I".';
            }
            $hasPdf = MediaAsset::whereKey($post->newsletter_pdf_media_id)->where('kind', 'pdf')->exists();
            $hasLink = trim((string) $post->newsletter_link) !== '';
            if ($hasPdf === $hasLink) {
                $errors['newsletter_pdf_media_id'] = 'Add either a PDF or a link, not both.';
            }
        } elseif ($post->blocks === [] || $post->blocks === null) {
            $errors['blocks'] = 'Add at least one block of content.';
        }

        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages($errors);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: PostService**

`cms/app/Content/PostService.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Content\Blocks\BlockNormalizer;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Models\PostVersion;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Carbon\CarbonInterface;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use InvalidArgumentException;

class PostService
{
    private const CONTENT_FIELDS = [
        'title', 'summary', 'blocks', 'grade_mode', 'grade_ids', 'event_date_start', 'event_date_end',
        'cover_media_id', 'newsletter_series_id', 'newsletter_term', 'newsletter_pdf_media_id', 'newsletter_link',
    ];

    public function __construct(
        private readonly BlockNormalizer $blocks,
        private readonly AuditLogger $audit,
    ) {}

    public function saveDraft(?Post $post, Section $section, array $data, Actor $actor, bool $isSuperAdmin): Post
    {
        if ($post !== null && $post->status !== PostStatus::Draft) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['status' => 'This post is not a draft. Use Publish to save changes.']);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $section, $data, $actor, $isSuperAdmin) {
            $post ??= new Post(['section' => $section, 'status' => PostStatus::Draft]);
            $before = $post->exists ? $post->snapshot() : null;
            if ($post->status === PostStatus::Draft) {
                $post->section = $section;
            }
            $this->fill($post, $data, $isSuperAdmin);
            $post->search_text = SearchText::fromPost($post);
            $post->save();

            return $this->recordChange($post, 'post.draft_saved', $actor, $before);
        });
    }

    public function publish(Post $post, array $data, PublishOptions $options, Actor $actor, bool $isSuperAdmin): Post
    {
        $original = $post->getAttributes();

        try {
            return $this->doPublish($post, $data, $options, $actor, $isSuperAdmin);
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            // A rejected publish must leave the in-memory model exactly as it was.
            $post->setRawAttributes($original, true);
            throw $e;
        }
    }

    private function doPublish(Post $post, array $data, PublishOptions $options, Actor $actor, bool $isSuperAdmin): Post
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $data, $options, $actor, $isSuperAdmin) {
            $now = CarbonImmutable::now();
            $before = $post->exists ? $post->snapshot() : null;
            $wasLive = $post->exists && in_array($post->adminStatus($now), ['live', 'hidden'], true);

            $this->fill($post, $data, $isSuperAdmin);
            $this->validateOptions($options, $now, $wasLive);

            if ($post->section === Section::Newsletter) {
                $post->academic_year = $data['newsletter_academic_year'] ?? $post->academic_year;
            }

            if ($wasLive) {
                $action = 'post.edited';
                $post->updated_at_public = $now;
                if (! $options->smallFix) {
                    $post->sort_at = $now;
                }
            } elseif ($options->publishAt !== null && $options->publishAt->greaterThan($now)) {
                $action = 'post.scheduled';
                $post->status = PostStatus::Scheduled;
                $post->publish_at = $options->publishAt;
                $post->first_published_at = $options->publishAt;
                $post->sort_at = $options->publishAt;
            } else {
                $action = 'post.published';
                $post->status = PostStatus::Published;
                $post->publish_at = $now;
                $post->first_published_at = $now;
                $post->sort_at = $now;
            }

            if ($post->section !== Section::Newsletter && $post->first_published_at !== null && ! $wasLive) {
                $post->academic_year = AcademicYear::forDate($post->first_published_at);
            }

            $post->remove_on = $options->removeOn;
            $post->pinned_until = $options->pinnedUntil;
            $post->slug ??= Str::limit(Str::slug($post->title), 160, '') ?: 'post';

            PostRules::assertPublishable($post);

            $post->search_text = SearchText::fromPost($post);
            $post->save();

            return $this->recordChange($post, $action, $actor, $before);
        });
    }

    public function unschedule(Post $post, Actor $actor): Post
    {
        if ($post->adminStatus() !== 'scheduled') {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['status' => 'Only scheduled posts can be unscheduled.']);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $actor) {
            $before = $post->snapshot();
            $post->status = PostStatus::Draft;
            $post->publish_at = null;
            $post->first_published_at = null;
            $post->sort_at = null;
            $post->academic_year = $post->section === Section::Newsletter ? $post->academic_year : null;
            $post->save();

            return $this->recordChange($post, 'post.unscheduled', $actor, $before);
        });
    }

    public function setPin(Post $post, ?CarbonImmutable $until, Actor $actor): Post
    {
        if ($until !== null && $until->lessThanOrEqualTo(CarbonImmutable::now())) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['pinned_until' => 'The unpin date must be in the future.']);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $until, $actor) {
            $before = $post->snapshot();
            $post->pinned_until = $until;
            $post->save();

            return $this->recordChange($post, $until === null ? 'post.unpinned' : 'post.pinned', $actor, $before);
        });
    }

    public function delete(Post $post, Actor $actor): void
    {
        DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $actor) {
            $before = $post->snapshot();
            $post->delete();
            $this->audit->record('post.deleted', $actor, $post, $before, null);
        });
    }

    public function restore(Post $post, Actor $actor): Post
    {
        if (! $post->trashed() || $post->purged_at !== null) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['post' => 'This post cannot be restored.']);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $actor) {
            $post->restore();
            $this->audit->record('post.restored', $actor, $post, null, $post->snapshot());

            return $post->refresh();
        });
    }

    public function restoreVersion(Post $post, PostVersion $version, Actor $actor): Post
    {
        abort_unless((int) $version->post_id === (int) $post->id, 404);

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($post, $version, $actor) {
            $before = $post->snapshot();
            foreach (self::CONTENT_FIELDS as $field) {
                if (array_key_exists($field, $version->data)) {
                    $post->{$field} = $version->data[$field];
                }
            }
            if (in_array($post->adminStatus(), ['live', 'hidden'], true)) {
                $post->updated_at_public = CarbonImmutable::now();
            }
            $post->search_text = SearchText::fromPost($post);
            $post->save();

            return $this->recordChange($post, 'post.version_restored', $actor, $before);
        });
    }

    public function purgeExpired(?CarbonInterface $now = null): int
    {
        $cutoff = CarbonImmutable::instance($now ?? CarbonImmutable::now())->subDays((int) config('cms.recycle_days'));
        $count = 0;

        Post::onlyTrashed()->whereNull('purged_at')->where('deleted_at', '<=', $cutoff)->each(function (Post $post) use (&$count) {
            DB::transaction(function () use ($post) {
                $before = $post->snapshot();
                PostVersion::where('post_id', $post->id)->delete();
                $post->forceFill([
                    'title' => '[removed]',
                    'summary' => null,
                    'blocks' => [],
                    'grade_mode' => 'none',
                    'grade_ids' => null,
                    'search_text' => null,
                    'cover_media_id' => null,
                    'newsletter_pdf_media_id' => null,
                    'newsletter_link' => null,
                    'purged_at' => CarbonImmutable::now(),
                ])->saveQuietly();
                $this->audit->record('post.purged', Actor::system(), $post, $before, null);
            });
            $count++;
        });

        return $count;
    }

    private function fill(Post $post, array $data, bool $isSuperAdmin): void
    {
        $title = trim((string) ($data['title'] ?? ''));
        if ($title === '' || mb_strlen($title) > config('cms.limits.title')) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['title' => 'Add a title (up to '.config('cms.limits.title').' characters).']);
        }
        $summary = trim((string) ($data['summary'] ?? ''));
        if (mb_strlen($summary) > config('cms.limits.summary')) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['summary' => 'The summary can be at most '.config('cms.limits.summary').' characters.']);
        }

        try {
            $grades = GradeScope::fromInput((array) ($data['grades'] ?? ['mode' => 'none']))->toStorage();
        } catch (InvalidArgumentException $e) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['grades' => $e->getMessage()]);
        }

        $post->title = $title;
        $post->summary = $summary === '' ? null : $summary;
        $post->blocks = $this->blocks->normalize((array) ($data['blocks'] ?? []), $isSuperAdmin, $post->exists ? ($post->getOriginal('blocks') ?? []) : []);
        $post->grade_mode = $grades['mode'];
        $post->grade_ids = $grades['ids'];

        $isEvent = $post->section === Section::Event;
        $isNewsletter = $post->section === Section::Newsletter;

        $post->event_date_start = $isEvent ? ($data['event_date_start'] ?? null) : null;
        $post->event_date_end = $isEvent ? ($data['event_date_end'] ?? null) : null;
        if ($isEvent && $post->event_date_start && $post->event_date_end && $post->event_date_end->lessThan($post->event_date_start)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['event_date_end' => 'The end date must be on or after the start date.']);
        }
        $post->cover_media_id = ($isEvent || $isNewsletter) ? ($data['cover_media_id'] ?? null) : null;
        $post->newsletter_series_id = $isNewsletter ? ($data['newsletter_series_id'] ?? null) : null;
        $post->newsletter_term = $isNewsletter ? (trim((string) ($data['newsletter_term'] ?? '')) ?: null) : null;
        $post->newsletter_pdf_media_id = $isNewsletter ? ($data['newsletter_pdf_media_id'] ?? null) : null;
        $post->newsletter_link = $isNewsletter ? (trim((string) ($data['newsletter_link'] ?? '')) ?: null) : null;
        if ($isNewsletter && isset($data['newsletter_academic_year'])) {
            $post->academic_year = $data['newsletter_academic_year'];
        }
        if ($post->newsletter_link !== null && ! in_array(strtolower((string) parse_url($post->newsletter_link, PHP_URL_SCHEME)), ['http', 'https'], true)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['newsletter_link' => 'The link must start with https://']);
        }
    }

    private function validateOptions(PublishOptions $options, CarbonImmutable $now, bool $wasLive): void
    {
        $errors = [];
        if ($options->publishAt !== null && $wasLive && $options->publishAt->greaterThan($now)) {
            $errors['publish_at'] = 'This post is already live, so it cannot be scheduled. Delete it or edit it now.';
        }
        if ($options->publishAt !== null && ! $wasLive && $options->publishAt->lessThanOrEqualTo($now)) {
            $errors['publish_at'] = 'The scheduled time has already passed. Choose a future time or publish now.';
        }
        $goesLiveAt = $options->publishAt && $options->publishAt->greaterThan($now) ? $options->publishAt : $now;
        if ($options->removeOn !== null && $options->removeOn->lessThanOrEqualTo($goesLiveAt)) {
            $errors['remove_on'] = 'The "remove from site" date must be after the post goes live.';
        }
        if ($options->pinnedUntil !== null && $options->pinnedUntil->lessThanOrEqualTo($now)) {
            $errors['pinned_until'] = 'The unpin date must be in the future.';
        }
        if ($errors !== []) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages($errors);
        }
    }

    private function recordChange(Post $post, string $action, Actor $actor, ?array $before): Post
    {
        $post->refresh();
        $after = $post->snapshot();
        PostVersion::create([
            'post_id' => $post->id,
            'version' => (int) PostVersion::where('post_id', $post->id)->lockForUpdate()->max('version') + 1,
            'data' => $after,
            'actor_name' => $actor->name,
            'created_at' => CarbonImmutable::now(),
        ]);
        $this->audit->record($action, $actor, $post, $before, $after);

        return $post;
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Content;

use App\Content\CalendarService;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\CalendarEntry;
use App\Models\CalendarType;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Tests\TestCase;

class CalendarServiceTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private function actor(): Actor
    {
        return new Actor('Ms. Priya', 1, 'editor', '1.1.1.1', 'UA', 's');
    }

    private function holiday(): int
    {
        return CalendarType::where('name', 'Holiday')->value('id');
    }

    public function test_create_update_delete_with_audit(): void
    {
        $service = app(CalendarService::class);
        $entry = $service->create(['title' => 'Eid holidays', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-03-16', 'end_date' => '2027-03-20', 'grades' => ['mode' => 'all'], 'note' => null], $this->actor());

        $this->assertSame('2027-03-20', $entry->end_date->format('Y-m-d'));
        $service->update($entry, ['title' => 'Eid al-Fitr holidays', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-03-16', 'end_date' => null, 'grades' => ['mode' => 'none'], 'note' => 'Tentative'], $this->actor());
        $this->assertNull($entry->fresh()->end_date);
        $service->delete($entry->fresh(), $this->actor());

        $this->assertSame(['calendar.created', 'calendar.updated', 'calendar.deleted'], AuditEntry::orderBy('id')->pluck('action')->all());
        $this->assertSame(0, CalendarEntry::count());
    }

    public function test_validation(): void
    {
        $service = app(CalendarService::class);
        foreach ([
            ['title' => '', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-01-01'],
            ['title' => 'X', 'calendar_type_id' => 999, 'start_date' => '2027-01-01'],
            ['title' => 'X', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => 'not-a-date'],
            ['title' => 'X', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-01-05', 'end_date' => '2027-01-01'],
            ['title' => 'X', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-01-05', 'note' => str_repeat('a', 501)],
        ] as $bad) {
            try {
                $service->create($bad, $this->actor());
                $this->fail('accepted: '.json_encode($bad));
            } catch (ValidationException) {
                $this->addToAssertionCount(1);
            }
        }
    }

    public function test_overlapping_scope_includes_ranges_crossing_month_edges(): void
    {
        $service = app(CalendarService::class);
        $service->create(['title' => 'Winter break', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2026-12-28', 'end_date' => '2027-01-03'], $this->actor());
        $service->create(['title' => 'Single day', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-01-15'], $this->actor());
        $service->create(['title' => 'Feb', 'calendar_type_id' => $this->holiday(), 'start_date' => '2027-02-01'], $this->actor());

        $jan = CalendarEntry::overlapping(CarbonImmutable::parse('2027-01-01'), CarbonImmutable::parse('2027-02-01'))->pluck('title')->all();
        $this->assertEqualsCanonicalizing(['Winter break', 'Single day'], $jan);
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Models/CalendarEntry.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Content\GradeScope;
use Carbon\CarbonInterface;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class CalendarEntry extends Model
{
    use SoftDeletes;

    protected $guarded = ['id'];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return ['start_date' => 'immutable_date', 'end_date' => 'immutable_date', 'grade_ids' => 'array'];
    }

    public function type(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(CalendarType::class, 'calendar_type_id');
    }

    public function scopeOverlapping(Builder $query, CarbonInterface $from, CarbonInterface $toExclusive): Builder
    {
        return $query
            ->where('start_date', '<', $toExclusive->format('Y-m-d'))
            ->whereRaw('COALESCE(end_date, start_date) >= ?', [$from->format('Y-m-d')]);
    }

    public function gradeScope(): GradeScope
    {
        return GradeScope::fromStorage($this->grade_mode, $this->grade_ids);
    }

    public function snapshot(): array
    {
        return [
            'title' => $this->title,
            'calendar_type_id' => (int) $this->calendar_type_id,
            'start_date' => $this->start_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'end_date' => $this->end_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'grade_mode' => $this->grade_mode,
            'grade_ids' => $this->grade_ids,
            'note' => $this->note,
        ];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: CalendarService**

`cms/app/Content/CalendarService.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Models\CalendarEntry;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use InvalidArgumentException;

class CalendarService
{
    public function __construct(private readonly AuditLogger $audit) {}

    public function create(array $data, Actor $actor): CalendarEntry
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $actor) {
            $entry = new CalendarEntry();
            $this->fill($entry, $data);
            $entry->save();
            $this->audit->record('calendar.created', $actor, $entry, null, $entry->snapshot());

            return $entry;
        });
    }

    public function update(CalendarEntry $entry, array $data, Actor $actor): CalendarEntry
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($entry, $data, $actor) {
            $before = $entry->snapshot();
            $this->fill($entry, $data);
            $entry->save();
            $this->audit->record('calendar.updated', $actor, $entry, $before, $entry->snapshot());

            return $entry;
        });
    }

    public function delete(CalendarEntry $entry, Actor $actor): void
    {
        DB::transaction(function () use ($entry, $actor) {
            $before = $entry->snapshot();
            $entry->delete();
            $this->audit->record('calendar.deleted', $actor, $entry, $before, null);
        });
    }

    private function fill(CalendarEntry $entry, array $data): void
    {
        $v = Validator::make($data, [
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:'.config('cms.limits.title')],
            'calendar_type_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:calendar_types,id'],
            'start_date' => ['required', 'date_format:Y-m-d'],
            'end_date' => ['nullable', 'date_format:Y-m-d', 'after_or_equal:start_date'],
            'note' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:'.config('cms.limits.calendar_note')],
        ], [
            'end_date.after_or_equal' => 'The end date must be on or after the start date.',
        ]);
        $valid = $v->validate();

        try {
            $grades = GradeScope::fromInput((array) ($data['grades'] ?? ['mode' => 'none']))->toStorage();
        } catch (InvalidArgumentException $e) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['grades' => $e->getMessage()]);
        }

        $entry->title = trim($valid['title']);
        $entry->calendar_type_id = (int) $valid['calendar_type_id'];
        $entry->start_date = $valid['start_date'];
        $entry->end_date = $valid['end_date'] ?? null;
        $entry->note = isset($valid['note']) && trim($valid['note']) !== '' ? trim($valid['note']) : null;
        $entry->grade_mode = $grades['mode'];
        $entry->grade_ids = $grades['ids'];
    }
}
```

### Task 10: Scheduled tasks (cron + visitor-triggered fallback)

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/app/Scheduling/DueTasks.php`, `cms/app/Console/Commands/RunDueTasks.php`, `cms/app/Http/Middleware/RunDueTasksAfterResponse.php`
- Modify: `cms/routes/console.php`, `cms/bootstrap/app.php`
- Test: `cms/tests/Feature/Scheduling/DueTasksTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PostService::purgeExpired` (Task 8); `AuditLogger`, `Actor::system()` (Task 3); `Post`, `PostStatus` (Task 5).
- Produces:
  - `DueTasks::run(?CarbonInterface $now = null): array{published: int, unpinned: int, purged: int}`.
  - Command `cms:due-tasks`.
  - Middleware `RunDueTasksAfterResponse`, appended to the `web` group; it runs `DueTasks` in `terminate()` at most once per 60 s using `Cache::add('cms:due-tasks:ran', true, 60)`.
  - New audit action `post.auto_published` (System actor).
  - Setting `scheduler.last_run_at` (ISO string) and `scheduler.last_source` (`cron|visitor`).

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Scheduling;

use App\Content\PostStatus;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Models\Setting;
use App\Scheduling\DueTasks;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
use Tests\TestCase;

class DueTasksTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->travelTo(CarbonImmutable::parse('2026-09-15 07:00:00'));
    }

    public function test_publishes_due_scheduled_posts_only(): void
    {
        $due = Post::factory()->scheduled(now()->subMinute())->create();
        $future = Post::factory()->scheduled(now()->addHour())->create();

        $result = app(DueTasks::class)->run();

        $this->assertSame(1, $result['published']);
        $this->assertSame(PostStatus::Published, $due->fresh()->status);
        $this->assertSame(PostStatus::Scheduled, $future->fresh()->status);
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.auto_published')->where('target_id', $due->id)->where('actor_name', 'System (scheduled)')->exists());
    }

    public function test_clears_expired_pins(): void
    {
        $expired = Post::factory()->published()->create(['pinned_until' => now()->subMinute()]);
        $active = Post::factory()->published()->create(['pinned_until' => now()->addDay()]);

        $this->assertSame(1, app(DueTasks::class)->run()['unpinned']);
        $this->assertNull($expired->fresh()->pinned_until);
        $this->assertNotNull($active->fresh()->pinned_until);
    }

    public function test_purges_recycle_bin(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();
        $post->delete();
        $this->travel(8)->days();

        $this->assertSame(1, app(DueTasks::class)->run()['purged']);
    }

    public function test_command_records_cron_source(): void
    {
        $this->artisan('cms:due-tasks')->assertSuccessful();
        $this->assertSame('cron', Setting::get('scheduler.last_source'));
    }

    public function test_visitor_fallback_runs_at_most_once_per_minute(): void
    {
        Cache::flush();
        Post::factory()->scheduled(now()->subMinute())->create();

        $this->get('/staff-cms/login')->assertOk();
        $this->assertSame(1, Post::where('status', 'published')->count());
        $this->assertSame('visitor', Setting::get('scheduler.last_source'));

        Post::factory()->scheduled(now()->subSecond())->create();
        $this->get('/staff-cms/login');
        $this->assertSame(1, Post::where('status', 'published')->count(), 'second request inside 60s must not run tasks');

        $this->travel(61)->seconds();
        $this->get('/staff-cms/login');
        $this->assertSame(2, Post::where('status', 'published')->count());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: DueTasks**

`cms/app/Scheduling/DueTasks.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Scheduling;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Content\PostStatus;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Carbon\CarbonInterface;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class DueTasks
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly PostService $posts,
        private readonly AuditLogger $audit,
    ) {}

    /** @return array{published: int, unpinned: int, purged: int} */
    public function run(?CarbonInterface $now = null): array
    {
        $now = CarbonImmutable::instance($now ?? CarbonImmutable::now());
        $published = 0;

        Post::where('status', PostStatus::Scheduled->value)->where('publish_at', '<=', $now)->each(function (Post $post) use (&$published) {
            DB::transaction(function () use ($post) {
                $before = $post->snapshot();
                $post->status = PostStatus::Published;
                $post->save();
                $this->audit->record('post.auto_published', Actor::system(), $post, $before, $post->snapshot());
            });
            $published++;
        });

        $unpinned = Post::whereNotNull('pinned_until')->where('pinned_until', '<=', $now)->update(['pinned_until' => null]);

        return [
            'published' => $published,
            'unpinned' => $unpinned,
            'purged' => $this->posts->purgeExpired($now),
        ];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Command, middleware, registration**

`cms/app/Console/Commands/RunDueTasks.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Setting;
use App\Scheduling\DueTasks;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class RunDueTasks extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'cms:due-tasks';

    protected $description = 'Publish scheduled posts, expire pins and empty the recycle bin';

    public function handle(DueTasks $tasks): int
    {
        $result = $tasks->run();
        Setting::put('scheduler.last_run_at', now()->toIso8601String());
        Setting::put('scheduler.last_source', 'cron');
        $this->info(json_encode($result));

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Middleware/RunDueTasksAfterResponse.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use App\Models\Setting;
use App\Scheduling\DueTasks;
use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;
use Throwable;

class RunDueTasksAfterResponse
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        return $next($request);
    }

    public function terminate(Request $request, Response $response): void
    {
        if (! Cache::add('cms:due-tasks:ran', true, 60)) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            app(DueTasks::class)->run();
            Setting::put('scheduler.last_run_at', now()->toIso8601String());
            Setting::put('scheduler.last_source', 'visitor');
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            Log::error('Visitor-triggered due tasks failed', ['exception' => $e]);
        }
    }
}
```

In `cms/routes/console.php` add:

```php
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schedule;

Schedule::command('cms:due-tasks')->everyMinute()->withoutOverlapping();
```

In `cms/bootstrap/app.php` inside `withMiddleware`, add:

```php
    $middleware->appendToGroup('web', \App\Http\Middleware\RunDueTasksAfterResponse::class);
```

Laravel's test client calls `terminate()` on terminable middleware after each request, which the test relies on. Cache TTL uses the travelled clock with the default `database`/`array` store. If `CACHE_STORE` is not set for tests, add `<env name="CACHE_STORE" value="array"/>` to `phpunit.xml`.

Note: `Cache::add` with the array store keeps values for the whole test process. `Cache::flush()` at the start of the test isolates it. Laravel resets the app per test, so other tests are not affected.

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Content\Section;
use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\Post;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class PostScreensTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->travelTo(CarbonImmutable::parse('2026-09-15 10:00:00'));
        $editor = Account::factory()->editor()->create();
        $this->actingAs($editor)->withSession(['session_version' => $editor->session_version]);
    }

    private function form(array $overrides = []): array
    {
        return array_merge([
            'actor_name' => 'Ms. Priya',
            'intent' => 'publish',
            'title' => 'Welcome back message',
            'summary' => '',
            'blocks_json' => json_encode([['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p>Welcome back!</p>']]),
            'grades' => ['mode' => 'all'],
            'publish_when' => 'now',
        ], $overrides);
    }

    public function test_dashboard_and_screens_render(): void
    {
        Post::factory()->scheduled(now()->addDay())->create(['title' => 'Scheduled one']);
        Post::factory()->published()->create(['title' => 'Pinned one', 'pinned_until' => now()->addDays(2)]);
        Post::factory()->draft()->create(['title' => 'Draft one']);

        $this->get(route('admin.dashboard'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Scheduled one')->assertSee('Pinned one')->assertSee('Draft one');
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.choose'))->assertOk()->assertSee(route('admin.posts.create', 'circular'), false);
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.create', 'circular'))->assertOk()->assertSee('name="actor_name"', false);
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.create', 'nonsense'))->assertNotFound();
    }

    public function test_publish_new_post_now(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'info'), $this->form())->assertRedirect();

        $post = Post::firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('live', $post->adminStatus());
        $this->assertSame('All grades', $post->gradeScope()->label());
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.published')->where('actor_name', 'Ms. Priya')->exists());
    }

    public function test_save_draft_then_schedule(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'circular'), $this->form(['intent' => 'draft']));
        $post = Post::firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame('draft', $post->adminStatus());

        $this->put(route('admin.posts.update', $post), $this->form(['publish_when' => 'schedule', 'publish_at' => '2026-09-20T07:00']))
            ->assertRedirect(route('admin.posts.edit', $post));
        $this->assertSame('2026-09-20 07:00:00', $post->fresh()->publish_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
    }

    public function test_name_is_required_and_never_echoed_back(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'info'), $this->form(['actor_name' => '']))->assertSessionHasErrors('actor_name');
        $this->assertSame(0, Post::count());

        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'info'), $this->form(['actor_name' => 'Typed Name', 'title' => '']))->assertSessionHasErrors('title');
        $page = $this->get(route('admin.posts.create', 'info'))->getContent();
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('Typed Name', $page);
    }

    public function test_edit_live_post_with_small_fix(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published(now()->subDays(3))->create();
        $sort = $post->sort_at;

        $this->put(route('admin.posts.update', $post), $this->form(['small_fix' => '1']))->assertRedirect();

        $this->assertEquals($sort, $post->fresh()->sort_at);
        $this->assertNotNull($post->fresh()->updated_at_public);
    }

    public function test_pin_checkbox_requires_unpin_date(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'info'), $this->form(['pin' => '1', 'pinned_until' => '']))->assertSessionHasErrors('pinned_until');
    }

    public function test_block_errors_are_returned_to_form(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'info'), $this->form(['blocks_json' => json_encode([['type' => 'link', 'label' => 'x', 'url' => 'javascript:1']])]))
            ->assertSessionHasErrors('blocks.0');
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.store', 'info'), $this->form(['blocks_json' => '{not json']))
            ->assertSessionHasErrors('blocks_json');
    }

    public function test_delete_duplicate_unschedule_unpin(): void
    {
        $live = Post::factory()->published()->create(['title' => 'Original', 'pinned_until' => now()->addDay()]);
        $scheduled = Post::factory()->scheduled(now()->addDay())->create();

        $this->post(route('admin.posts.duplicate', $live), ['actor_name' => 'A'])->assertRedirect();
        $this->assertTrue(Post::where('title', 'Copy of Original')->where('status', 'draft')->exists());

        $this->post(route('admin.posts.unpin', $live), ['actor_name' => 'A'])->assertRedirect();
        $this->assertNull($live->fresh()->pinned_until);

        $this->post(route('admin.posts.unschedule', $scheduled), ['actor_name' => 'A'])->assertRedirect();
        $this->assertSame('draft', $scheduled->fresh()->adminStatus());

        $this->delete(route('admin.posts.destroy', $live), ['actor_name' => ''])->assertSessionHasErrors('actor_name');
        $this->delete(route('admin.posts.destroy', $live), ['actor_name' => 'A'])->assertRedirect(route('admin.posts.index'));
        $this->assertSoftDeleted($live);
    }

    public function test_index_filters_by_section_and_status(): void
    {
        Post::factory()->section(Section::Circular)->published()->create(['title' => 'Live circular']);
        Post::factory()->section(Section::Info)->draft()->create(['title' => 'Draft info']);

        $this->get(route('admin.posts.index', ['section' => 'circular']))->assertSee('Live circular')->assertDontSee('Draft info');
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.index', ['status' => 'draft']))->assertSee('Draft info')->assertDontSee('Live circular');
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.index', ['q' => 'circular']))->assertSee('Live circular');
    }

    public function test_preview_renders_without_saving(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.preview'), $this->form(['section' => 'info', 'title' => 'Preview title']))
            ->assertOk()->assertSee('Preview title')->assertSee('Welcome back!');
        $this->assertSame(0, Post::count());
    }

    public function test_editor_cannot_open_deleted_post_for_editing(): void
    {
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();
        $post->delete();
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.edit', $post->id))->assertNotFound();
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\CalendarEntry;
use App\Models\CalendarType;
use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Tests\Support\Fixtures;
use Tests\TestCase;

class UploadAndCalendarScreensTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        Storage::fake('media');
        $editor = Account::factory()->editor()->create();
        $this->actingAs($editor)->withSession(['session_version' => $editor->session_version]);
    }

    public function test_upload_returns_json(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.media.upload'), ['kind' => 'image', 'file' => Fixtures::uploaded(Fixtures::jpegWithExif(600, 400), 'a.jpg')])
            ->assertOk()->assertJsonStructure(['id', 'kind', 'original_name', 'preview_url']);
    }

    public function test_upload_rejection_is_422_with_message(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.media.upload'), ['kind' => 'pdf', 'file' => Fixtures::uploaded(Fixtures::jpegWithExif(10, 10), 'x.pdf')])
            ->assertStatus(422)->assertJsonPath('message', fn ($m) => str_contains($m, 'not a PDF'));
    }

    public function test_media_edit_crops(): void
    {
        $id = $this->post(route('admin.media.upload'), ['kind' => 'image', 'file' => Fixtures::uploaded(Fixtures::jpegWithExif(1000, 500), 'a.jpg')])->json('id');

        $this->post(route('admin.media.edit', $id), ['crop' => ['x' => 0, 'y' => 0, 'w' => 500, 'h' => 500], 'max_width' => 300])
            ->assertOk()->assertJsonPath('id', $id);
        $this->assertSame(2, MediaAsset::find($id)->rev);
    }

    public function test_calendar_crud(): void
    {
        $type = CalendarType::where('name', 'Exam')->value('id');

        $this->get(route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => 2026, 'month' => 10]))->assertOk();

        $this->post(route('admin.calendar.store'), ['actor_name' => '', 'title' => 'Half yearly', 'calendar_type_id' => $type, 'start_date' => '2026-10-04'])
            ->assertSessionHasErrors('actor_name');

        $this->post(route('admin.calendar.store'), ['actor_name' => 'Ms. Priya', 'title' => 'Half yearly', 'calendar_type_id' => $type, 'start_date' => '2026-10-04', 'end_date' => '2026-10-15', 'grades' => ['mode' => 'none']])
            ->assertRedirect(route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => 2026, 'month' => 10]));
        $entry = CalendarEntry::firstOrFail();

        $this->get(route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => 2026, 'month' => 10]))->assertSee('Half yearly');

        $this->put(route('admin.calendar.update', $entry), ['actor_name' => 'Ms. Priya', 'title' => 'Half yearly exams', 'calendar_type_id' => $type, 'start_date' => '2026-10-04'])
            ->assertRedirect();
        $this->assertSame('Half yearly exams', $entry->fresh()->title);

        $this->delete(route('admin.calendar.destroy', $entry), ['actor_name' => 'Ms. Priya'])->assertRedirect();
        $this->assertSoftDeleted($entry);
    }

    public function test_recycle_bin_lists_deleted_posts_for_editor(): void
    {
        $post = \App\Models\Post::factory()->published()->create(['title' => 'Binned']);
        $post->delete();
        $this->get(route('admin.recycle.index'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Binned');
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Admin;

use App\Content\PublishOptions;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

class SavePostRequest extends ActorRequest
{
    protected function fieldRules(): array
    {
        return [
            'intent' => ['required', 'in:draft,publish'],
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:'.config('cms.limits.title')],
            'summary' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:'.config('cms.limits.summary')],
            'blocks_json' => ['nullable', 'string', 'json'],
            'grades' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'grades.mode' => ['nullable', 'in:none,all,range,specific'],
            'grades.from' => ['nullable', 'integer'],
            'grades.to' => ['nullable', 'integer'],
            'grades.ids' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'grades.ids.*' => ['integer'],
            'event_date_start' => ['nullable', 'date_format:Y-m-d'],
            'event_date_end' => ['nullable', 'date_format:Y-m-d'],
            'cover_media_id' => ['nullable', 'integer'],
            'newsletter_series_id' => ['nullable', 'integer'],
            'newsletter_academic_year' => ['nullable', 'regex:/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/'],
            'newsletter_term' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:40'],
            'newsletter_pdf_media_id' => ['nullable', 'integer'],
            'newsletter_link' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:2000'],
            'publish_when' => ['nullable', 'in:now,schedule'],
            'publish_at' => ['nullable', 'required_if:publish_when,schedule', 'date_format:Y-m-d\TH:i'],
            'remove_on' => ['nullable', 'date_format:Y-m-d\TH:i'],
            'pin' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
            'pinned_until' => ['nullable', 'required_if:pin,1', 'date_format:Y-m-d\TH:i'],
            'small_fix' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
        ];
    }

    public function messages(): array
    {
        return parent::messages() + [
            'publish_at.required_if' => 'Choose the date and time to publish.',
            'pinned_until.required_if' => 'Pinned posts need an "unpin on" date.',
            'blocks_json.json' => 'The content could not be read. Reload the page and try again.',
        ];
    }

    private function dt(?string $value): ?CarbonImmutable
    {
        return $value ? CarbonImmutable::createFromFormat('Y-m-d\TH:i', $value, config('app.timezone'))->setSecond(0) : null;
    }

    public function postData(): array
    {
        $v = $this->validated();

        return [
            'title' => $v['title'],
            'summary' => $v['summary'] ?? null,
            'blocks' => isset($v['blocks_json']) ? (array) json_decode($v['blocks_json'], true) : [],
            'grades' => $v['grades'] ?? ['mode' => 'none'],
            'event_date_start' => $v['event_date_start'] ?? null,
            'event_date_end' => $v['event_date_end'] ?? null,
            'cover_media_id' => $v['cover_media_id'] ?? null,
            'newsletter_series_id' => $v['newsletter_series_id'] ?? null,
            'newsletter_academic_year' => $v['newsletter_academic_year'] ?? null,
            'newsletter_term' => $v['newsletter_term'] ?? null,
            'newsletter_pdf_media_id' => $v['newsletter_pdf_media_id'] ?? null,
            'newsletter_link' => $v['newsletter_link'] ?? null,
        ];
    }

    public function publishOptions(): PublishOptions
    {
        $v = $this->validated();

        return new PublishOptions(
            publishAt: ($v['publish_when'] ?? 'now') === 'schedule' ? $this->dt($v['publish_at'] ?? null) : null,
            removeOn: $this->dt($v['remove_on'] ?? null),
            pinnedUntil: $this->boolean('pin') ? $this->dt($v['pinned_until'] ?? null) : null,
            smallFix: $this->boolean('small_fix'),
        );
    }
}
```
