`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PostController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Content\Blocks\BlockNormalizer;
use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Content\PostStatus;
use App\Content\Section;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\SavePostRequest;
use App\Models\Grade;
use App\Models\NewsletterSeries;
use App\Models\Post;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class PostController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private readonly PostService $posts) {}

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $now = CarbonImmutable::now();
        $query = Post::query()->orderByDesc('updated_at');

        if ($section = Section::tryFrom((string) $request->query('section'))) {
            $query->ofSection($section);
        }
        if ($q = trim((string) $request->query('q'))) {
            $query->where('title', 'like', '%'.str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\%', '\_'], $q).'%');
        }
        $status = (string) $request->query('status');
        match ($status) {
            'draft' => $query->where('status', PostStatus::Draft->value),
            'scheduled' => $query->where('status', PostStatus::Scheduled->value)->where('publish_at', '>', $now),
            'live' => $query->publicEligible($now),
            'hidden' => $query->where('status', '!=', PostStatus::Draft->value)->whereNotNull('remove_on')->where('remove_on', '<=', $now),
            default => null,
        };

        return view('admin.posts.index', ['posts' => $query->paginate(25)->withQueryString(), 'sections' => Section::cases()]);
    }

    public function choose()
    {
        return view('admin.posts.choose', ['sections' => Section::cases()]);
    }

    public function create(string $section)
    {
        $section = Section::tryFrom($section) ?? abort(404);

        return view('admin.posts.form', $this->formData(new Post(['section' => $section, 'status' => PostStatus::Draft])));
    }

    public function store(SavePostRequest $request, string $section)
    {
        $section = Section::tryFrom($section) ?? abort(404);
        $isSuper = $request->user()->isSuperAdmin();

        if ($request->validated('intent') === 'draft') {
            $post = $this->posts->saveDraft(null, $section, $request->postData(), $request->actor(), $isSuper);
            return redirect()->route('admin.posts.edit', $post)->with('status', 'Draft saved.')->with('clear_autosave', 'new-'.$section->value);
        }

        $post = new Post(['section' => $section, 'status' => PostStatus::Draft]);
        $post = $this->posts->publish($post, $request->postData(), $request->publishOptions(), $request->actor(), $isSuper);

        return redirect()->route('admin.posts.edit', $post)->with('status', $this->publishedMessage($post))->with('clear_autosave', 'new-'.$section->value);
    }

    public function edit(Post $post)
    {
        abort_if($post->purged_at !== null, 404);

        return view('admin.posts.form', $this->formData($post));
    }

    public function update(SavePostRequest $request, Post $post)
    {
        $isSuper = $request->user()->isSuperAdmin();

        if ($request->validated('intent') === 'draft') {
            $this->posts->saveDraft($post, $post->section, $request->postData(), $request->actor(), $isSuper);
            return redirect()->route('admin.posts.edit', $post)->with('status', 'Draft saved.')->with('clear_autosave', 'post-'.$post->id);
        }

        $post = $this->posts->publish($post, $request->postData(), $request->publishOptions(), $request->actor(), $isSuper);

        return redirect()->route('admin.posts.edit', $post)->with('status', $this->publishedMessage($post))->with('clear_autosave', 'post-'.$post->id);
    }

    public function destroy(NameOnlyRequest $request, Post $post)
    {
        $this->posts->delete($post, $request->actor());

        return redirect()->route('admin.posts.index')->with('status', '"'.$post->title.'" moved to the recycle bin. IT can restore it within '.config('cms.recycle_days').' days.');
    }

    public function preview(Request $request, BlockNormalizer $normalizer)
    {
        $section = Section::tryFrom((string) $request->input('section')) ?? abort(404);
        try {
            $blocks = $normalizer->normalize((array) json_decode((string) $request->input('blocks_json', '[]'), true), $request->user()->isSuperAdmin());
        } catch (ValidationException $e) {
            return response()->view('admin.posts.preview', ['errors_list' => $e->errors(), 'post' => null], 422);
        }

        $post = new Post(['section' => $section, 'title' => (string) $request->input('title'), 'summary' => $request->input('summary')]);
        $post->blocks = $blocks;

        return view('admin.posts.preview', ['post' => $post, 'errors_list' => []]);
    }

    private function publishedMessage(Post $post): string
    {
        return match ($post->adminStatus()) {
            'scheduled' => 'Scheduled for '.$post->publish_at->format('j M Y, g:i A').'.',
            default => 'Published. It is now live on the website.',
        };
    }

    private function formData(Post $post): array
    {
        return [
            'post' => $post,
            'grades' => Grade::ordered()->visible()->get(),
            'series' => NewsletterSeries::visible()->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'autosaveKey' => $post->exists ? 'post-'.$post->id : 'new-'.$post->section->value,
        ];
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PostActionController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Content\PublishOptions;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;
use App\Models\Post;

class PostActionController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private readonly PostService $posts) {}

    public function duplicate(NameOnlyRequest $request, Post $post)
    {
        $data = [
            'title' => mb_substr('Copy of '.$post->title, 0, config('cms.limits.title')),
            'summary' => $post->summary,
            'blocks' => $post->blocks,
            // A stored range is an expanded id list, so re-submit it as "specific" (same label).
            'grades' => ['mode' => $post->grade_mode === 'range' ? 'specific' : $post->grade_mode, 'ids' => $post->grade_ids ?? []],
            'event_date_start' => $post->event_date_start?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'event_date_end' => $post->event_date_end?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'cover_media_id' => $post->cover_media_id,
            'newsletter_series_id' => $post->newsletter_series_id,
            'newsletter_academic_year' => $post->academic_year,
            'newsletter_term' => $post->newsletter_term,
            'newsletter_pdf_media_id' => $post->newsletter_pdf_media_id,
            'newsletter_link' => $post->newsletter_link,
        ];
        $copy = $this->posts->saveDraft(null, $post->section, $data, $request->actor(), true);

        return redirect()->route('admin.posts.edit', $copy)->with('status', 'Copy created as a draft. Change what you need, then publish.');
    }

    public function unschedule(NameOnlyRequest $request, Post $post)
    {
        $this->posts->unschedule($post, $request->actor());

        return back()->with('status', 'Unscheduled. The post is a draft again.');
    }

    public function unpin(NameOnlyRequest $request, Post $post)
    {
        $this->posts->setPin($post, null, $request->actor());

        return back()->with('status', 'Unpinned.');
    }
}
```

Note: `duplicate` passes `$isSuperAdmin = true` deliberately. The copy only repeats blocks that already exist, so an editor gains nothing new, and custom-page blocks survive the copy. Subsequent edits by an editor still use the normal rule.

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/MediaUploadController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Media\FileUploader;
use App\Media\ImageProcessor;
use App\Media\UploadRejected;
use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class MediaUploadController extends Controller
{
    public function upload(Request $request, FileUploader $uploader)
    {
        $request->validate(['kind' => ['required', 'in:image,pdf,video'], 'file' => ['required', 'file']]);

        try {
            $asset = $uploader->upload($request->file('file'), $request->input('kind'));
        } catch (UploadRejected $e) {
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }

        return response()->json($this->payload($asset));
    }

    public function edit(Request $request, MediaAsset $asset, ImageProcessor $images)
    {
        abort_unless($asset->kind === 'image', 404);
        $data = $request->validate([
            'crop' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'crop.x' => ['required_with:crop', 'integer', 'min:0'],
            'crop.y' => ['required_with:crop', 'integer', 'min:0'],
            'crop.w' => ['required_with:crop', 'integer', 'min:1'],
            'crop.h' => ['required_with:crop', 'integer', 'min:1'],
            'max_width' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:50', 'max:2560'],
        ]);

        try {
            $images->edit($asset, $data['crop'] ?? null, $data['max_width'] ?? null);
        } catch (UploadRejected $e) {
            return response()->json(['message' => $e->getMessage()], 422);
        }

        return response()->json($this->payload($asset->refresh()));
    }

    private function payload(MediaAsset $asset): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $asset->id,
            'kind' => $asset->kind,
            'original_name' => $asset->original_name,
            'preview_url' => match ($asset->kind) {
                'image' => $asset->url('400.jpg'),
                'pdf' => $asset->url('file.pdf'),
                'video' => $asset->url('file.mp4'),
            },
            'width' => $asset->width,
            'height' => $asset->height,
        ];
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CalendarController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Content\CalendarService;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\CalendarEntryRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;
use App\Models\CalendarEntry;
use App\Models\CalendarType;
use App\Models\Grade;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class CalendarController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private readonly CalendarService $calendar) {}

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $year = (int) $request->query('year', now()->year);
        $month = (int) $request->query('month', now()->month);
        abort_unless($year >= 2000 && $year <= 2100 && $month >= 1 && $month <= 12, 404);

        $start = CarbonImmutable::create($year, $month, 1);
        $end = $start->addMonth();

        return view('admin.calendar.index', [
            'monthStart' => $start,
            'entries' => CalendarEntry::with('type')->overlapping($start, $end)->orderBy('start_date')->get(),
            'types' => CalendarType::visible()->orderBy('id')->get(),
            'grades' => Grade::ordered()->visible()->get(),
            'editing' => $request->query('edit') ? CalendarEntry::find((int) $request->query('edit')) : null,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(CalendarEntryRequest $request)
    {
        $entry = $this->calendar->create($request->entryData(), $request->actor());

        return redirect()->route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => $entry->start_date->year, 'month' => $entry->start_date->month])->with('status', 'Added to the calendar.');
    }

    public function update(CalendarEntryRequest $request, CalendarEntry $entry)
    {
        $entry = $this->calendar->update($entry, $request->entryData(), $request->actor());

        return redirect()->route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => $entry->start_date->year, 'month' => $entry->start_date->month])->with('status', 'Calendar entry updated.');
    }

    public function destroy(NameOnlyRequest $request, CalendarEntry $entry)
    {
        $this->calendar->delete($entry, $request->actor());

        return back()->with('status', 'Calendar entry deleted.');
    }
}
```

```php
    Route::put('calendar/{entry}', [CalendarController::class, 'update'])->name('calendar.update');
    Route::delete('calendar/{entry}', [CalendarController::class, 'destroy'])->name('calendar.destroy');

    Route::get('recycle-bin', [RecycleBinController::class, 'index'])->name('recycle.index');

    if (app()->environment('testing')) {

});
```

The tests above need the views, which Task 12 creates. Do not commit yet. Task 12 runs these tests and commits both tasks together. A reviewer gates Tasks 11 + 12 as one unit.

---

### Task 12: Editor admin views + block editor script (unstyled)

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/resources/views/admin/partials/actor-name.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/partials/errors.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/partials/blocks.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/posts/index.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/posts/choose.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/posts/form.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/posts/preview.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/calendar/index.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/recycle/index.blade.php`, `cms/public/admin/editor.js`
- Modify: `cms/resources/views/admin/layout.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/dashboard.blade.php`
- Test: Task 11 tests + `cms/tests/Feature/Admin/ActorNameRenderingTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: controller view data from Task 11:
  - `dashboard`: `recent`, `scheduled`, `pinned`, `drafts`
  - `posts.index`: `posts`, `sections`
  - `posts.form`: `post`, `grades`, `series`, `autosaveKey`
  - `calendar.index`: `monthStart`, `entries`, `types`, `grades`, `editing`
  - `recycle.index`: `posts`, `days`
- Produces:
  - **`data-*` hooks** Claude Design will restyle: `data-screen="…"` on each `<main>` child, `data-region="…"` for areas, `data-state="…"` for statuses.
  - **`public/admin/editor.js` global contract:**
    - Reads `<script type="application/json" id="blocks-data">`.
    - Writes hidden `blocks_json` on submit.
    - Upload endpoint from `data-upload-url`; CSRF token from `<meta name="csrf-token">`.
    - localStorage key `cms-autosave:{autosaveKey}`, cleared when the page has `data-clear-autosave="{key}"`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the rendering test**

`cms/tests/Feature/Admin/ActorNameRenderingTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\CalendarEntry;
use App\Models\CalendarType;
use App\Models\Post;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class ActorNameRenderingTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_every_actor_name_input_is_blank_and_not_autocompleted(): void
    {
        $editor = Account::factory()->editor()->create();
        $this->actingAs($editor)->withSession(['session_version' => $editor->session_version, '_old_input' => ['actor_name' => 'Remembered']]);

        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create(['pinned_until' => now()->addDay()]);
        CalendarEntry::create(['title' => 'Exam', 'calendar_type_id' => CalendarType::value('id'), 'start_date' => now()->format('Y-m-d')]);

        foreach ([
            route('admin.posts.create', 'info'),
            route('admin.posts.edit', $post),
            route('admin.dashboard'),
            route('admin.calendar.index'),
            route('admin.posts.index'),
        ] as $url) {
            $html = $this->get($url)->assertOk()->getContent();
            preg_match_all('/<input[^>]*name="actor_name"[^>]*>/', $html, $m);
            $this->assertNotEmpty($m[0], "no actor_name input on {$url}");
            foreach ($m[0] as $input) {
                $this->assertStringNotContainsString('value=', $input, $url);
                $this->assertStringContainsString('autocomplete="off"', $input, $url);
                $this->assertStringContainsString('required', $input, $url);
            }
            $this->assertStringNotContainsString('Remembered', $html);
        }
    }
}
```

```blade
            <ul data-region="pdfs">@foreach ($block['files'] as $file)@if ($m = $media[$file['media_id']] ?? null)<li><a href="{{ $m->url('file.pdf') }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">{{ $file['label'] }}</a> · <a href="{{ $m->url('file.pdf') }}?download=1">Download</a></li>@endif @endforeach</ul>
            @break
        @case('link')<p><a href="{{ $block['url'] }}" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" data-region="link-button">{{ $block['label'] }}</a></p>@break
        @case('youtube')
            @if ($block['mode'] === 'player')
                <div data-region="youtube-player" data-video-id="{{ $block['video_id'] }}"><a href="{{ $block['url'] }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img src="https://i.ytimg.com/vi/{{ $block['video_id'] }}/hqdefault.jpg" alt="Play video on YouTube" loading="lazy"></a></div>
            @else
                <p><a href="{{ $block['url'] }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Watch on YouTube</a></p>
            @endif
            @break
        @case('video')
            @if ($m = $media[$block['media_id']] ?? null)<video controls preload="metadata" src="{{ $m->url('file.mp4') }}"></video>@if($block['caption'])<p>{{ $block['caption'] }}</p>@endif @endif
            @break
        @case('table')
            <div data-region="table-scroll"><table>@foreach ($block['rows'] as $i => $row)<tr>@foreach ($row as $cell)@if ($i === 0 && $block['has_header'])<th scope="col">{{ $cell }}</th>@else<td>{!! nl2br(e($cell)) !!}</td>@endif @endforeach</tr>@endforeach</table></div>
            @break
        @case('html_page')
            @php($page = \App\Models\HtmlPage::find($block['html_page_id']))
            @if ($page && \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route::has('pages.sandboxed'))
                @if ($block['mode'] === 'embed')<iframe src="{{ route('pages.sandboxed', $page->token) }}" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-popups" title="{{ $page->title }}" loading="lazy"></iframe>@else<p><a href="{{ route('pages.sandboxed', $page->token) }}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">{{ $page->title }}</a></p>@endif
            @endif
            @break
    @endswitch
    </div>
@endforeach
```

```blade
@extends('admin.layout')
@section('title', 'Calendar')
@section('content')
@php($prev = $monthStart->subMonth())
@php($next = $monthStart->addMonth())
<div data-screen="calendar">
    <h1>Calendar: {{ $monthStart->format('F Y') }}</h1>
    <p data-region="month-nav">
        <a href="{{ route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => $prev->year, 'month' => $prev->month]) }}">← {{ $prev->format('F') }}</a>
        <a href="{{ route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => $next->year, 'month' => $next->month]) }}">{{ $next->format('F') }} →</a>
    </p>
    @include('admin.partials.errors')

    <table data-region="month-grid">
        <thead><tr>@foreach (['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'] as $d)<th>{{ $d }}</th>@endforeach</tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        @php($day = $monthStart->startOfWeek(\Carbon\CarbonInterface::SUNDAY))
        @while ($day < $next->endOfWeek(\Carbon\CarbonInterface::SATURDAY))
            <tr>
            @for ($i = 0; $i < 7; $i++)
                @php($dayEntries = $entries->filter(fn ($e) => $e->start_date->lte($day) && ($e->end_date ?? $e->start_date)->gte($day)))
                <td data-state="{{ $day->month === $monthStart->month ? 'in-month' : 'out-of-month' }}">
                    <span>{{ $day->day }}</span>
                    @foreach ($dayEntries as $e)<div data-type="{{ \Illuminate\Support\Str::slug($e->type->name) }}"><a href="{{ route('admin.calendar.index', ['year' => $monthStart->year, 'month' => $monthStart->month, 'edit' => $e->id]) }}">{{ $e->title }}</a></div>@endforeach
                </td>
                @php($day = $day->addDay())
            @endfor
            </tr>
        @endwhile
        </tbody>
    </table>

    @php($e = $editing)
    <form method="post" action="{{ $e ? route('admin.calendar.update', $e) : route('admin.calendar.store') }}" data-region="calendar-form">

        <h2>{{ $e ? 'Edit entry' : 'Add an entry' }}</h2>
        <label>Title <input name="title" required maxlength="150" value="{{ old('title', $e?->title) }}"></label>

        <label>Start date <input type="date" name="start_date" required value="{{ old('start_date', $e?->start_date?->format('Y-m-d')) }}"></label>
        <label>End date (optional) <input type="date" name="end_date" value="{{ old('end_date', $e?->end_date?->format('Y-m-d')) }}"></label>
        <label>Grades <select name="grades[mode]"><option value="none">Not grade-specific</option><option value="all" @selected(old('grades.mode', $e?->grade_mode) === 'all')>All grades</option></select></label>
```

```blade
@extends('admin.layout')
@section('title', 'Recycle bin')
@section('content')
<div data-screen="recycle-bin">
    <h1>Recycle bin</h1>
    <p>Deleted posts stay here for {{ $days }} days, then are removed permanently. Only IT (Super admin) can restore them.</p>
    <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Section</th><th>Deleted</th><th>Removed permanently</th><th></th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        @forelse ($posts as $post)
            <tr data-state="deleted">
                <td>{{ $post->title }}</td><td>{{ $post->section->label() }}</td>
                <td>{{ $post->deleted_at->format('j M Y, g:i A') }}</td>
                <td>{{ $post->deleted_at->addDays($days)->format('j M Y') }}</td>
                <td>@if (auth()->user()->isSuperAdmin() && Route::has('admin.recycle.restore'))<form method="post" action="{{ route('admin.recycle.restore', $post->id) }}">@csrf @include('admin.partials.actor-name')<button>Restore</button></form>@endif</td>
            </tr>
        @empty
            <tr data-state="empty"><td colspan="5">The recycle bin is empty.</td></tr>
        @endforelse
        </tbody>
    </table>
</div>
@endsection
```

### Task 12b: `public/admin/editor.js` + manual check + commit (Tasks 11–12)

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/public/admin/editor.js`
- Test: manual browser checklist (Step 2) + full PHPUnit suite

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: DOM contract from `posts/form.blade.php`:
  - `#post-form[data-upload-url][data-media-edit-url][data-autosave-key][data-updated-at][data-super-admin]`
  - `#block-list`, `#add-block-type`, `#add-block`, `#blocks_json`, `#blocks-data`, `#media-data`, `#preview-button[data-preview-url]`
  - `input[data-upload-for][data-kind]`, `[data-media-preview]`, `[data-grade-mode]`, `[data-region="autosave-status"]`, `[data-clear-autosave]`
- Produces: block JSON in the normalized shapes from Task 7 (`table` is always sent as `rows`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the script**

`cms/public/admin/editor.js`:

```js
/* DPS Millennium CMS block editor. Plain ES2017, no build step. Styling is Claude Design's job. */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  var form = document.getElementById('post-form');
  var clearKey = document.querySelector('[data-clear-autosave]');
  if (clearKey) {
    try { localStorage.removeItem('cms-autosave:' + clearKey.getAttribute('data-clear-autosave')); } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (!form) { return; }

  var csrf = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute('content');
  var isSuper = form.getAttribute('data-super-admin') === '1';
  var list = document.getElementById('block-list');
  var mediaCache = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('media-data').textContent || '{}');
  var blocks = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('blocks-data').textContent || '[]');
  var autosaveKey = 'cms-autosave:' + form.getAttribute('data-autosave-key');
  var statusEl = document.querySelector('[data-region="autosave-status"]');

  function el(tag, attrs, children) {
    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    Object.keys(attrs || {}).forEach(function (k) {
      if (k === 'text') { node.textContent = attrs[k]; }
      else if (k.indexOf('on') === 0) { node.addEventListener(k.slice(2), attrs[k]); }
      else if (attrs[k] !== null && attrs[k] !== undefined) { node.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); }
    });
    (children || []).forEach(function (c) { if (c) { node.appendChild(c); } });
    return node;
  }

  function say(message) { if (statusEl) { statusEl.textContent = message; } }

  /* ---------- uploads ---------- */
  function upload(file, kind) {
    var data = new FormData();
    data.append('file', file);
    data.append('kind', kind);
    say('Uploading ' + file.name + '…');
    return fetch(form.getAttribute('data-upload-url'), {
      method: 'POST', body: data, credentials: 'same-origin',
      headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrf, 'Accept': 'application/json' }
    }).then(function (res) {
      return res.json().catch(function () { return { message: 'Upload failed. Try again.' }; }).then(function (json) {
        if (!res.ok) { throw new Error(json.message || 'Upload failed. Try again.'); }
        mediaCache[json.id] = json;
        say('Uploaded ' + json.original_name + '.');
        return json;
      });
    }).catch(function (err) { say(''); alertInline(err.message); throw err; });
  }

  function alertInline(message) {
    var box = el('p', { 'class': 'error', role: 'alert', text: message });
    list.parentNode.insertBefore(box, list);
    setTimeout(function () { box.remove(); }, 12000);
  }

  function mediaPreview(id) {
    var m = mediaCache[id];
    if (!m) { return el('span', { text: id ? 'File #' + id : 'Nothing uploaded yet' }); }
    if (m.kind === 'image') { return el('img', { src: m.preview_url, alt: m.original_name, width: '160' }); }
    return el('a', { href: m.preview_url, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener', text: m.original_name });
  }

  /* ---------- single-field uploads (cover photo, newsletter PDF) ---------- */
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('input[data-upload-for]'), function (input) {
    var target = form.querySelector('input[name="' + input.getAttribute('data-upload-for') + '"]');
    var preview = form.querySelector('[data-media-preview="' + input.getAttribute('data-upload-for') + '"]');
    function show() { preview.innerHTML = ''; preview.appendChild(mediaPreview(target.value)); }
    show();
    input.addEventListener('change', function () {
      if (!input.files[0]) { return; }
      upload(input.files[0], input.getAttribute('data-kind')).then(function (m) { target.value = m.id; show(); input.value = ''; });
    });
  });

  /* ---------- grade mode toggle ---------- */
  function syncGrades() {
    var checked = form.querySelector('input[name="grades[mode]"]:checked');
    var mode = checked ? checked.value : 'none';
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-grade-mode]'), function (box) {
      box.hidden = box.getAttribute('data-grade-mode') !== mode;
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(box.querySelectorAll('input,select'), function (i) { i.disabled = box.hidden; });
    });
  }
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.querySelectorAll('input[name="grades[mode]"]'), function (r) { r.addEventListener('change', syncGrades); });
  syncGrades();

  /* ---------- block defaults ---------- */
  var LABELS = { text: 'Text', image: 'Photo', gallery: 'Photo gallery', pdf: 'PDF file(s)', link: 'Link button', youtube: 'YouTube video', video: 'Short video', table: 'Table', html_page: 'Custom HTML page' };

  function blank(type) {
    switch (type) {
      case 'text': return { type: 'text', html: '' };
      case 'image': return { type: 'image', media_id: null, caption: '' };
      case 'gallery': return { type: 'gallery', items: [] };
      case 'pdf': return { type: 'pdf', files: [] };
      case 'link': return { type: 'link', label: '', url: '' };
      case 'youtube': return { type: 'youtube', url: '', mode: 'link' };
      case 'video': return { type: 'video', media_id: null, caption: '' };
      case 'table': return { type: 'table', has_header: true, rows: [['', ''], ['', '']] };
      case 'html_page': return { type: 'html_page', html_page_id: null, mode: 'link' };
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* ---------- per-type editors: each returns a DOM node and mutates block in place ---------- */
  function textEditor(block) {
    var area = el('div', { contenteditable: 'true', 'data-region': 'rich-text', role: 'textbox', 'aria-multiline': 'true' });
    area.innerHTML = block.html || '<p></p>';
    area.addEventListener('input', function () { block.html = area.innerHTML; });
    function cmd(name, value) { return function () { area.focus(); document.execCommand(name, false, value); block.html = area.innerHTML; }; }
    var bar = el('div', { 'data-region': 'text-toolbar' }, [
      el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Bold', onclick: cmd('bold') }),
      el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Italic', onclick: cmd('italic') }),
      el('button', { type: 'button', text: '• List', onclick: cmd('insertUnorderedList') }),
      el('button', { type: 'button', text: '1. List', onclick: cmd('insertOrderedList') }),
      el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Link', onclick: function () {
        var url = prompt('Paste the full web address (https://…)');
        if (url) { cmd('createLink', url)(); }
      } }),
      el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Remove link', onclick: cmd('unlink') })
    ]);
    area.addEventListener('paste', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData('text/plain');
      document.execCommand('insertText', false, text);
    });
    return el('div', {}, [bar, area]);
  }

  function fileInput(kind, multiple, onFiles) {
    var accept = { image: 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/heic', pdf: 'application/pdf', video: 'video/mp4' }[kind];
    var input = el('input', { type: 'file', accept: accept });
    if (multiple) { input.multiple = true; }
    input.addEventListener('change', function () {
      var files = Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files);
      input.value = '';
      files.reduce(function (p, f) { return p.then(function () { return upload(f, kind).then(onFiles).catch(function () {}); }); }, Promise.resolve());
    });
    return input;
  }

  function cropControls(block) {
    var fields = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 0, h: 0, max_width: '' };
    var inputs = Object.keys(fields).map(function (k) {
      var i = el('input', { type: 'number', min: '0', 'aria-label': k, placeholder: k === 'max_width' ? 'max width px' : k });
      i.addEventListener('input', function () { fields[k] = i.value; });
      return el('label', { text: k === 'max_width' ? 'Max width ' : k.toUpperCase() + ' ' }, [i]);
    });
    var apply = el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Apply crop / resize', onclick: function () {
      if (!block.media_id) { return; }
      var body = {};
      if (+fields.w > 0 && +fields.h > 0) { body.crop = { x: +fields.x, y: +fields.y, w: +fields.w, h: +fields.h }; }
      if (+fields.max_width > 0) { body.max_width = +fields.max_width; }
      fetch(form.getAttribute('data-media-edit-url').replace('__ID__', block.media_id), {
        method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrf, 'Accept': 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(body)
      }).then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (j) { if (!r.ok) { throw new Error(j.message || 'Could not edit the photo.'); } return j; }); })
        .then(function (j) { mediaCache[j.id] = j; render(); say('Photo updated.'); })
        .catch(function (err) { alertInline(err.message); });
    } });
    var m = mediaCache[block.media_id];
    return el('details', { 'data-region': 'crop' }, [el('summary', { text: 'Crop or resize' }),
      el('p', { text: m && m.width ? 'Photo is ' + m.width + ' × ' + m.height + ' px.' : '' })].concat(inputs).concat([apply]));
  }

  function captionInput(obj) {
    var i = el('input', { type: 'text', maxlength: '300', placeholder: 'Caption (optional)' });
    i.value = obj.caption || '';
    i.addEventListener('input', function () { obj.caption = i.value; });
    return i;
  }

  function imageEditor(block, kind) {
    var wrap = el('div');
    wrap.appendChild(mediaPreview(block.media_id));
    wrap.appendChild(el('label', { text: block.media_id ? 'Replace ' : 'Choose ' }, [fileInput(kind, false, function (m) { block.media_id = m.id; render(); })]));
    wrap.appendChild(captionInput(block));
    if (kind === 'image' && block.media_id) { wrap.appendChild(cropControls(block)); }
    return wrap;
  }

  function galleryEditor(block) {
    var wrap = el('div');
    var ul = el('ul');
    block.items.forEach(function (item, idx) {
      ul.appendChild(el('li', {}, [mediaPreview(item.media_id), captionInput(item),
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Remove photo', onclick: function () { block.items.splice(idx, 1); render(); } })]));
    });
    wrap.appendChild(ul);
    wrap.appendChild(el('label', { text: 'Add photos (you can select many) ' }, [fileInput('image', true, function (m) { block.items.push({ media_id: m.id, caption: '' }); render(); })]));
    return wrap;
  }

  function pdfEditor(block) {
    var wrap = el('div');
    block.files.forEach(function (file, idx) {
      var label = el('input', { type: 'text', maxlength: '150', placeholder: 'Label shown to parents' });
      label.value = file.label || '';
      label.addEventListener('input', function () { file.label = label.value; });
      wrap.appendChild(el('div', {}, [mediaPreview(file.media_id), label,
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Remove PDF', onclick: function () { block.files.splice(idx, 1); render(); } })]));
    });
    wrap.appendChild(el('label', { text: 'Add PDF(s) ' }, [fileInput('pdf', true, function (m) { block.files.push({ media_id: m.id, label: m.original_name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, '') }); render(); })]));
    return wrap;
  }

  function textField(obj, key, attrs) {
    var i = el('input', attrs);
    i.value = obj[key] || '';
    i.addEventListener('input', function () { obj[key] = i.value; });
    return i;
  }

  function radioPair(obj, key, options) {
    var name = 'r' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
    return el('div', {}, options.map(function (o) {
      var r = el('input', { type: 'radio', name: name, value: o[0] });
      r.checked = obj[key] === o[0];
      r.addEventListener('change', function () { obj[key] = o[0]; });
      return el('label', { text: ' ' + o[1] + ' ' }, [r]);
    }));
  }

  function parsePasted(text) {
    var rows = [], row = [], cell = '', i = 0, quoted = false;
    text = text.replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n');
    for (; i < text.length; i++) {
      var ch = text[i];
      if (quoted) {
        if (ch === '"' && text[i + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; i++; }
        else if (ch === '"') { quoted = false; }
        else { cell += ch; }
      } else if (ch === '"' && cell === '') { quoted = true; }
      else if (ch === '\t') { row.push(cell); cell = ''; }
      else if (ch === '\n') { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); row = []; cell = ''; }
      else { cell += ch; }
    }
    if (cell !== '' || row.length) { row.push(cell); rows.push(row); }
    var width = rows.reduce(function (w, r) { return Math.max(w, r.length); }, 0);
    return rows.map(function (r) { while (r.length < width) { r.push(''); } return r; });
  }

  function tableEditor(block) {
    var wrap = el('div');
    var header = el('input', { type: 'checkbox' });
    header.checked = !!block.has_header;
    header.addEventListener('change', function () { block.has_header = header.checked; });
    wrap.appendChild(el('label', { text: ' First row is a heading' }, [header]));

    var paste = el('textarea', { rows: '3', placeholder: 'Copy cells in Excel or Google Sheets, click here and paste (Ctrl+V). This replaces the table below.' });
    paste.addEventListener('paste', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var rows = parsePasted((e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData('text/plain'));
      if (rows.length) { block.rows = rows; render(); say('Table pasted: ' + rows.length + ' rows.'); }
    });
    wrap.appendChild(paste);

    var table = el('table', { 'data-region': 'table-grid' });
    block.rows.forEach(function (row, r) {
      var tr = el('tr');
      row.forEach(function (value, c) {
        var input = el('input', { type: 'text', 'aria-label': 'Row ' + (r + 1) + ' column ' + (c + 1) });
        input.value = value;
        input.addEventListener('input', function () { block.rows[r][c] = input.value; });
        tr.appendChild(el('td', {}, [input]));
      });
      table.appendChild(tr);
    });
    wrap.appendChild(el('div', { 'data-region': 'table-scroll' }, [table]));
    wrap.appendChild(el('button', { type: 'button', text: '+ Row', onclick: function () { block.rows.push(block.rows[0].map(function () { return ''; })); render(); } }));
    wrap.appendChild(el('button', { type: 'button', text: '+ Column', onclick: function () { block.rows.forEach(function (r) { r.push(''); }); render(); } }));
    wrap.appendChild(el('button', { type: 'button', text: '− Last row', onclick: function () { if (block.rows.length > 1) { block.rows.pop(); render(); } } }));
    wrap.appendChild(el('button', { type: 'button', text: '− Last column', onclick: function () { if (block.rows[0].length > 1) { block.rows.forEach(function (r) { r.pop(); }); render(); } } }));
    return wrap;
  }

  function editorFor(block) {
    switch (block.type) {
      case 'text': return textEditor(block);
      case 'image': return imageEditor(block, 'image');
      case 'video': return imageEditor(block, 'video');
      case 'gallery': return galleryEditor(block);
      case 'pdf': return pdfEditor(block);
      case 'link': return el('div', {}, [
        el('label', { text: 'Button label ' }, [textField(block, 'label', { type: 'text', maxlength: '100' })]),
        el('label', { text: 'Web address ' }, [textField(block, 'url', { type: 'url', placeholder: 'https://' })])]);
      case 'youtube': return el('div', {}, [
        el('label', { text: 'YouTube link ' }, [textField(block, 'url', { type: 'url', placeholder: 'https://youtu.be/…' })]),
        radioPair(block, 'mode', [['link', 'Show as a link'], ['player', 'Show the video player']])]);
      case 'html_page':
        if (!isSuper) { return el('p', { text: 'Custom page #' + block.html_page_id + ' (only IT can change this block; you can move or remove it).' }); }
        return el('div', {}, [
          el('label', { text: 'Custom page ID ' }, [textField(block, 'html_page_id', { type: 'number', min: '1' })]),
          radioPair(block, 'mode', [['link', 'Link'], ['embed', 'Embed on the page']])]);
    }
    return el('p', { text: 'Unknown block' });
  }

  /* ---------- list rendering ---------- */
  function render() {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!blocks.length) { list.appendChild(el('p', { 'data-state': 'empty', text: 'No content yet. Add a block below.' })); }
    blocks.forEach(function (block, idx) {
      var controls = el('div', { 'data-region': 'block-controls' }, [
        el('strong', { text: (idx + 1) + '. ' + (LABELS[block.type] || block.type) }),
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: '↑ Up', disabled: idx === 0 ? 'disabled' : null, onclick: function () { move(idx, -1); } }),
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: '↓ Down', disabled: idx === blocks.length - 1 ? 'disabled' : null, onclick: function () { move(idx, 1); } }),
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Remove', onclick: function () { if (confirm('Remove this ' + (LABELS[block.type] || 'block') + '?')) { blocks.splice(idx, 1); render(); } } })
      ]);
      list.appendChild(el('section', { 'data-block-editor': block.type }, [controls, editorFor(block)]));
    });
  }

  function move(idx, delta) {
    var target = idx + delta;
    if (target < 0 || target >= blocks.length) { return; }
    var tmp = blocks[idx]; blocks[idx] = blocks[target]; blocks[target] = tmp;
    render();
  }

  document.getElementById('add-block').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var select = document.getElementById('add-block-type');
    var block = blank(select.value);
    if (!block) { select.focus(); return; }
    blocks.push(block);
    select.value = '';
    render();
    list.lastElementChild.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  });

  /* ---------- submit / preview ---------- */
  function serialize() { document.getElementById('blocks_json').value = JSON.stringify(blocks); }

  form.addEventListener('submit', serialize);

  document.getElementById('preview-button').addEventListener('click', function () {
    serialize();
    var temp = el('form', { method: 'post', action: this.getAttribute('data-preview-url'), target: '_blank' });
    ['_token', 'section', 'title', 'summary', 'blocks_json'].forEach(function (name) {
      var src = form.querySelector('[name="' + name + '"]');
      if (src) { temp.appendChild(el('input', { type: 'hidden', name: name, value: src.value })); }
    });
    document.body.appendChild(temp);
    temp.submit();
    temp.remove();
  });

  /* ---------- autosave to this browser (not the server) ---------- */
  function snapshot() {
    serialize();
    var fields = {};
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.elements, function (f) {
      if (!f.name || f.name === 'actor_name' || f.name === '_token' || f.name === '_method' || f.type === 'file') { return; }
      if ((f.type === 'checkbox' || f.type === 'radio') && !f.checked) { return; }
      fields[f.name] = fields[f.name] === undefined ? f.value : [].concat(fields[f.name], f.value);
    });
    return { savedAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), fields: fields, media: mediaCache };
  }

  function autosave() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(autosaveKey, JSON.stringify(snapshot()));
      say('Unsaved changes kept on this computer at ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString() + '. Remember to Save draft or Publish.');
    } catch (e) { /* storage full or blocked: ignore */ }
  }

  function restore(saved) {
    Object.keys(saved.fields).forEach(function (name) {
      var values = [].concat(saved.fields[name]);
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(form.querySelectorAll('[name="' + name + '"]'), function (f) {
        if (f.type === 'checkbox' || f.type === 'radio') { f.checked = values.indexOf(f.value) !== -1; }
        else if (name !== 'blocks_json') { f.value = values[0]; }
      });
    });
    Object.keys(saved.media || {}).forEach(function (id) { mediaCache[id] = saved.media[id]; });
    blocks = JSON.parse(saved.fields.blocks_json || '[]');
    syncGrades();
    render();
    say('Unsaved changes restored.');
  }

  try {
    var saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(autosaveKey) || 'null');
    if (saved && saved.savedAt > +form.getAttribute('data-updated-at')) {
      var bar = el('p', { role: 'status', 'data-region': 'restore-autosave' }, [
        el('span', { text: 'You have unsaved changes from ' + new Date(saved.savedAt * 1000).toLocaleString() + '. ' }),
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Restore them', onclick: function () { restore(saved); bar.remove(); } }),
        el('button', { type: 'button', text: 'Discard', onclick: function () { localStorage.removeItem(autosaveKey); bar.remove(); } })
      ]);
      form.insertBefore(bar, form.firstChild);
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  setInterval(autosave, 10000);
  form.addEventListener('focusout', autosave);

  render();
})();
```

Note: `el()` skips attributes whose value is `null`, so `disabled: null` leaves a button enabled.

### Task 13: Super admin: change log, version history, recycle restore, security

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/AuditController.php`, `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/VersionController.php`, `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SecurityController.php`, `cms/app/Audit/Diff.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/audit/index.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/audit/show.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/posts/versions.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/security/edit.blade.php`

- Produces:

    - Change log: `audit.index`, `audit.show` (`{entry}`), `audit.verify`
    - Post versions: `posts.versions` (`{post}`), `posts.versions.restore` (`{post}`, `{version}`)
    - Recycle bin: `recycle.restore` (`{id}`)
    - Security: `security.edit`, `security.editor-password`, `security.own-password`, `security.logout-all`
  - `Diff::between(?array $before, ?array $after): array<string, array{before: mixed, after: mixed}>` (only changed top-level keys).

`cms/tests/Feature/Admin/SuperAdminAuditTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Audit\Diff;
use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Content\PublishOptions;
use App\Content\Section;
use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Tests\TestCase;

class SuperAdminAuditTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private function as(string $role): Account
    {
        $a = $role === 'super' ? Account::factory()->superAdmin()->create() : Account::factory()->editor()->create();
        $this->actingAs($a)->withSession(['session_version' => $a->session_version]);
        return $a;
    }

    public function test_editor_is_forbidden_from_super_admin_screens(): void
    {
        $this->as('editor');
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();
        $this->get(route('admin.audit.index'))->assertForbidden();
        $this->get(route('admin.posts.versions', $post))->assertForbidden();
        $this->get(route('admin.security.edit'))->assertForbidden();
        $post->delete();
        $this->post(route('admin.recycle.restore', $post->id), ['actor_name' => 'X'])->assertForbidden();
    }

    public function test_change_log_lists_filters_and_shows_diff(): void
    {
        $this->as('super');
        $actor = new Actor('Ms. Priya', 1, 'editor', '10.1.1.1', 'Firefox', 's1');
        app(AuditLogger::class)->record('settings.updated', $actor, null, ['homepage_count.info' => 5, 'same' => 1], ['homepage_count.info' => 6, 'same' => 1]);
        app(AuditLogger::class)->record('calendar.created', new Actor('Mr. Khan', 1, 'editor', '10.1.1.2', 'Chrome', 's2'), null, null, ['title' => 'Exam']);

        $this->get(route('admin.audit.index'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Ms. Priya')->assertSee('Mr. Khan');
        $this->get(route('admin.audit.index', ['name' => 'Khan']))->assertSee('Mr. Khan')->assertDontSee('Ms. Priya');
        $this->get(route('admin.audit.index', ['action' => 'settings.updated']))->assertSee('Ms. Priya')->assertDontSee('Mr. Khan');

        $entry = AuditEntry::where('action', 'settings.updated')->first();
        $this->get(route('admin.audit.show', $entry))->assertOk()->assertSee('homepage_count.info')->assertSee('10.1.1.1')->assertSee('Firefox')->assertDontSee('>same<', false);
    }

    public function test_diff_only_reports_changes(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(['b' => ['before' => 1, 'after' => 2], 'c' => ['before' => null, 'after' => 3]], Diff::between(['a' => 1, 'b' => 1], ['a' => 1, 'b' => 2, 'c' => 3]));
        $this->assertSame(['a' => ['before' => 1, 'after' => null]], Diff::between(['a' => 1], null));
    }

    public function test_verify_integrity_button(): void
    {
        $this->as('super');
        app(AuditLogger::class)->record('settings.updated', Actor::system());
        $this->post(route('admin.audit.verify'))->assertRedirect()->assertSessionHas('status', fn ($s) => str_contains($s, 'intact'));

        DB::table('audit_entries')->where('action', 'settings.updated')->update(['actor_name' => 'forged']);
        $this->post(route('admin.audit.verify'))->assertSessionHas('status', fn ($s) => str_contains($s, 'ALTERED'));
    }

    public function test_versions_list_and_restore(): void
    {
        $this->as('super');
        $service = app(PostService::class);
        $actor = new Actor('A', 1, 'editor', null, null, null);
        $post = $service->saveDraft(null, Section::Info, ['title' => 'V1', 'blocks' => [['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p>x</p>']]], $actor, false);
        $post = $service->publish($post, ['title' => 'V2', 'blocks' => [['type' => 'text', 'html' => '<p>x</p>']]], new PublishOptions(), $actor, false);

        $this->get(route('admin.posts.versions', $post))->assertOk()->assertSee('V1')->assertSee('V2');

        $v1 = $post->versions()->where('version', 1)->first();
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.versions.restore', [$post, $v1]), ['actor_name' => ''])->assertSessionHasErrors('actor_name');
        $this->post(route('admin.posts.versions.restore', [$post, $v1]), ['actor_name' => 'IT'])->assertRedirect(route('admin.posts.edit', $post));
        $this->assertSame('V1', $post->fresh()->title);
    }

    public function test_recycle_restore(): void
    {
        $this->as('super');
        $post = Post::factory()->published()->create();
        $post->delete();

        $this->post(route('admin.recycle.restore', $post->id), ['actor_name' => 'IT'])->assertRedirect(route('admin.recycle.index'));
        $this->assertFalse($post->fresh()->trashed());
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'post.restored')->where('actor_name', 'IT')->exists());
    }
}
```

`cms/tests/Feature/Admin/SecurityTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Tests\TestCase;

class SecurityTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private Account $super;
    private Account $editor;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->editor = Account::factory()->editor()->create(['username' => 'editor']);
        $this->super = Account::factory()->superAdmin()->create(['username' => 'superadmin']);
        $this->actingAs($this->super)->withSession(['session_version' => $this->super->session_version]);
    }

    public function test_page_renders(): void
    {
        $this->get(route('admin.security.edit'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Change the shared Editor password');
    }

    public function test_change_editor_password_logs_out_editors(): void
    {
        $before = $this->editor->session_version;

        $this->post(route('admin.security.editor-password'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'password' => 'short', 'password_confirmation' => 'short'])->assertSessionHasErrors('password');
        $this->post(route('admin.security.editor-password'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'password' => 'new-editor-pass-1', 'password_confirmation' => 'new-editor-pass-1'])->assertRedirect();

        $this->editor->refresh();
        $this->assertTrue(Hash::check('new-editor-pass-1', $this->editor->password));
        $this->assertSame($before + 1, $this->editor->session_version);
        $entry = AuditEntry::where('action', 'security.editor_password_changed')->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('new-editor-pass-1', (string) $entry->after_json);
    }

    public function test_change_own_password_requires_current_and_keeps_session(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.security.own-password'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'current_password' => 'wrong', 'password' => 'new-super-pass-12', 'password_confirmation' => 'new-super-pass-12'])
            ->assertSessionHasErrors('current_password');

        $this->post(route('admin.security.own-password'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'current_password' => 'correct-horse-battery', 'password' => 'new-super-pass-12', 'password_confirmation' => 'new-super-pass-12'])
            ->assertRedirect();

        $this->get(route('admin.security.edit'))->assertOk();
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'security.own_password_changed')->exists());
    }

    public function test_logout_all_bumps_every_account(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.security.logout-all'), ['actor_name' => 'IT'])->assertRedirect(route('admin.login'));
        $this->assertSame(2, $this->editor->fresh()->session_version);
        $this->assertSame(2, $this->super->fresh()->session_version);
        $this->assertTrue(AuditEntry::where('action', 'security.force_logout')->exists());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Diff helper and controllers**

`cms/app/Audit/Diff.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Audit;

final class Diff
{
    /** @return array<string, array{before: mixed, after: mixed}> */
    public static function between(?array $before, ?array $after): array
    {
        $before ??= [];
        $after ??= [];
        $out = [];
        foreach (array_unique(array_merge(array_keys($before), array_keys($after))) as $key) {
            $b = $before[$key] ?? null;
            $a = $after[$key] ?? null;
            if ($b !== $a) {
                $out[$key] = ['before' => $b, 'after' => $a];
            }
        }

        return $out;
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/AuditController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Audit\AuditChain;
use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Audit\Diff;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class AuditController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $query = AuditEntry::query()->orderByDesc('id');

        if ($name = trim((string) $request->query('name'))) {
            $query->where('actor_name', 'like', '%'.str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\%', '\_'], $name).'%');
        }
        if ($action = $request->query('action')) {
            $query->where('action', $action);
        }
        if ($from = $request->query('from')) {
            $query->where('occurred_at', '>=', $from.' 00:00:00');
        }
        if ($to = $request->query('to')) {
            $query->where('occurred_at', '<=', $to.' 23:59:59');
        }
        if ($postId = $request->query('post')) {
            $query->where('target_type', 'Post')->where('target_id', (int) $postId);
        }

        return view('admin.audit.index', [
            'entries' => $query->paginate(50)->withQueryString(),
            'actions' => AuditEntry::query()->select('action')->distinct()->orderBy('action')->pluck('action'),
        ]);
    }

    public function show(AuditEntry $entry)
    {
        return view('admin.audit.show', ['entry' => $entry, 'diff' => Diff::between($entry->beforeData(), $entry->afterData())]);
    }

    public function verify(Request $request, AuditChain $chain, AuditLogger $audit)
    {
        $broken = $chain->firstBrokenId();
        $audit->record('audit.verified', Actor::fromRequest($request, null), null, null, ['first_broken_id' => $broken]);

        return back()->with('status', $broken === null
            ? 'Change log intact: no entries have been altered.'
            : "WARNING: change log ALTERED at or before entry #{$broken}. Tell the school management and keep a backup copy.");
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Models\PostVersion;

class VersionController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Post $post)
    {
        return view('admin.posts.versions', ['post' => $post, 'versions' => $post->versions()->get()]);
    }

    public function restore(NameOnlyRequest $request, Post $post, PostVersion $version, PostService $posts)
    {
        $posts->restoreVersion($post, $version, $request->actor());

        return redirect()->route('admin.posts.edit', $post)->with('status', "Version {$version->version} restored.");
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SecurityController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;
use App\Models\Account;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class SecurityController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private readonly AuditLogger $audit) {}

    public function edit()
    {
        $sessions = DB::table('sessions')->whereNotNull('user_id')->orderByDesc('last_activity')->limit(50)->get()
            ->map(fn ($s) => (object) [
                'account' => Account::find($s->user_id)?->username ?? 'unknown',
                'ip' => $s->ip_address,
                'agent' => $s->user_agent,
                'last' => \Carbon\CarbonImmutable::createFromTimestamp($s->last_activity, config('app.timezone')),
            ]);

        return view('admin.security.edit', ['sessions' => $sessions]);
    }

    public function editorPassword(NameOnlyRequest $request)
    {
        $data = $request->validate(['password' => ['required', 'string', 'min:12', 'confirmed']]);
        $editor = Account::where('role', Account::EDITOR)->firstOrFail();
        $editor->password = $data['password'];
        $editor->password_changed_at = now();
        $editor->save();
        $editor->bumpSessionVersion();
        $this->audit->record('security.editor_password_changed', $request->actor(), $editor, null, ['changed' => true]);

        return back()->with('status', 'Editor password changed. Everyone using the Editor login has been logged out. Share the new password with staff privately.');
    }

    public function ownPassword(NameOnlyRequest $request)
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'current_password' => ['required', 'string'],
            'password' => ['required', 'string', 'min:12', 'confirmed'],
        ]);
        $account = $request->user();
        if (! Hash::check($data['current_password'], $account->password)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['current_password' => 'Your current password is incorrect.']);
        }
        $account->password = $data['password'];
        $account->password_changed_at = now();
        $account->save();
        $account->bumpSessionVersion();
        $request->session()->regenerate();
        $request->session()->put('session_version', $account->fresh()->session_version);
        $this->audit->record('security.own_password_changed', $request->actor(), $account, null, ['changed' => true]);

        return back()->with('status', 'Your password has been changed. Other devices using this login were logged out.');
    }

    public function logoutAll(NameOnlyRequest $request)
    {
        $actor = $request->actor();
        Account::query()->each(fn (Account $a) => $a->bumpSessionVersion());
        DB::table('sessions')->delete();
        $this->audit->record('security.force_logout', $actor, null, null, ['accounts' => Account::count()]);
        Auth::logout();
        $request->session()->invalidate();

        return redirect()->route('admin.login');
    }
}
```

Add a `restore` method to `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/RecycleBinController.php`:

```php
    public function restore(\App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest $request, int $id, \App\Content\PostService $posts)
    {
        $post = Post::onlyTrashed()->whereNull('purged_at')->findOrFail($id);
        $posts->restore($post, $request->actor());

        return redirect()->route('admin.recycle.index')->with('status', '"'.$post->title.'" restored. It is live again if it was live before.');
    }
```

```php
    Route::middleware('role:super_admin')->group(function () {
        Route::get('change-log', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AuditController::class, 'index'])->name('audit.index');
        Route::get('change-log/{entry}', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AuditController::class, 'show'])->name('audit.show');

        Route::post('posts/{post}/versions/{version}/restore', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\VersionController::class, 'restore'])->name('posts.versions.restore');

        Route::post('recycle-bin/{id}/restore', [RecycleBinController::class, 'restore'])->whereNumber('id')->name('recycle.restore');

        Route::get('security', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\SecurityController::class, 'edit'])->name('security.edit');
        Route::post('security/editor-password', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\SecurityController::class, 'editorPassword'])->name('security.editor-password');
        Route::post('security/own-password', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\SecurityController::class, 'ownPassword'])->name('security.own-password');
        Route::post('security/logout-all', [\App\Http\Controllers\Admin\SecurityController::class, 'logoutAll'])->name('security.logout-all');

        if (app()->environment('testing')) {
            Route::get('_role-probe', fn () => 'ok');
        }
    });
```

Register this group **before** `Route::get('posts/{post}', …)` is irrelevant because paths differ (`posts/{post}/versions`); no reordering is needed.

```blade
        @include('admin.partials.actor-name')
        <button>Log out everyone</button>
    </form>

    <h2>Active sessions</h2>
    <table data-region="sessions">
        <thead><tr><th>Login</th><th>IP</th><th>Device / browser</th><th>Last active</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        @forelse ($sessions as $s)
            <tr><td>{{ $s->account }}</td><td>{{ $s->ip }}</td><td>{{ \Illuminate\Support\Str::limit($s->agent, 80) }}</td><td>{{ $s->last->diffForHumans() }}</td></tr>
        @empty
            <tr data-state="empty"><td colspan="4">No active sessions.</td></tr>
        @endforelse
        </tbody>
    </table>
</div>
@endsection
```

### Task 14: Super admin: settings, sandboxed custom HTML pages, system status

**Files:**
- Create: `cms/app/Content/SettingsService.php`, `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SettingsController.php`, `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/HtmlPageController.php`, `cms/app/Http/Controllers/SandboxedPageController.php`, `cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SystemController.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/settings/edit.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/pages/index.blade.php`, `cms/resources/views/admin/system/show.blade.php`
- Modify: `cms/routes/admin.php`, `cms/routes/web.php`, `cms/config/filesystems.php` (add `pages` disk)
- Test: `cms/tests/Feature/Admin/SettingsTest.php`, `cms/tests/Feature/Admin/HtmlPagesTest.php`, `cms/tests/Feature/Admin/SystemStatusTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Setting`, `Grade`, `CalendarType`, `NewsletterSeries` (Task 4); `HtmlPage` (Task 7); `AuditLogger`, `Actor` (Task 3); `NameOnlyRequest` (Task 11).
- Produces:
  - `SettingsService::update(array $input, Actor $actor): void`. Input keys:
    - `homepage_count` (`info|circular|event|newsletter` → int 1–20)
    - `academic_year_start_month` (1–12)
    - `grades` (list of `{id?, label, position, hidden}`)
    - `calendar_types` (list of `{id?, name, hidden}`)
    - `newsletter_series` (list of `{id?, name, hidden}`)
    - New rows have no `id`; rows are never deleted.
  - **Route names:**
    - Settings: `settings.edit`, `settings.update`
    - Custom pages: `pages.index`, `pages.store`, `pages.destroy` (`{page}`)
    - System: `system.show`
    - Public: `pages.sandboxed` (`GET /pages/{token}`, not under the admin prefix)
  - Disk `pages` at `storage/app/html-pages` (files named `{token}.html`).

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\Grade;
use App\Models\NewsletterSeries;
use App\Models\Setting;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Tests\TestCase;

class SettingsTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $a = Account::factory()->superAdmin()->create();
        $this->actingAs($a)->withSession(['session_version' => $a->session_version]);
    }

    private function payload(array $overrides = []): array
    {
        return array_replace_recursive([
            'actor_name' => 'IT',
            'homepage_count' => ['info' => 6, 'circular' => 4, 'event' => 8, 'newsletter' => 4],
            'academic_year_start_month' => 4,
            'grades' => Grade::ordered()->get()->map(fn ($g) => ['id' => $g->id, 'label' => $g->label, 'position' => $g->position, 'hidden' => $g->hidden ? 1 : 0])->all(),
            'calendar_types' => [],
            'newsletter_series' => [['name' => 'Kindergarten', 'hidden' => 0]],
        ], $overrides);
    }

    public function test_renders_and_updates_with_audit(): void
    {
        $this->get(route('admin.settings.edit'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Homepage');

        $this->put(route('admin.settings.update'), $this->payload())->assertRedirect(route('admin.settings.edit'));

        $this->assertSame(6, Setting::get('homepage_count.info'));
        $this->assertTrue(NewsletterSeries::where('name', 'Kindergarten')->exists());
        $entry = AuditEntry::where('action', 'settings.updated')->firstOrFail();
        $this->assertSame(5, $entry->beforeData()['homepage_count']['info']);
        $this->assertSame(6, $entry->afterData()['homepage_count']['info']);
    }

    public function test_rename_and_hide_grade_add_new(): void
    {
        $payload = $this->payload();
        $payload['grades'][0]['label'] = 'Nursery';
        $payload['grades'][1]['hidden'] = 1;
        $payload['grades'][] = ['label' => 'KG3', 'position' => 35, 'hidden' => 0];

        $this->put(route('admin.settings.update'), $payload)->assertRedirect();

        $this->assertTrue(Grade::where('label', 'Nursery')->exists());
        $this->assertTrue((bool) Grade::where('label', 'KG1')->value('hidden'));
        $this->assertSame(['Nursery', 'KG1', 'KG2', 'KG3'], array_slice(Grade::ordered()->pluck('label')->all(), 0, 4));
    }

    public function test_validation(): void
    {
        $this->put(route('admin.settings.update'), $this->payload(['homepage_count' => ['info' => 0]]))->assertSessionHasErrors('homepage_count.info');
        $this->put(route('admin.settings.update'), $this->payload(['academic_year_start_month' => 13]))->assertSessionHasErrors('academic_year_start_month');
        $dup = $this->payload();
        $dup['grades'][1]['label'] = $dup['grades'][0]['label'];
        $this->put(route('admin.settings.update'), $dup)->assertSessionHasErrors('grades');
        $this->put(route('admin.settings.update'), $this->payload(['actor_name' => '']))->assertSessionHasErrors('actor_name');
    }

    public function test_editor_forbidden(): void
    {
        $e = Account::factory()->editor()->create();
        $this->actingAs($e)->withSession(['session_version' => $e->session_version]);
        $this->get(route('admin.settings.edit'))->assertForbidden();
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature\Admin;

use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\HtmlPage;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Tests\TestCase;

class HtmlPagesTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        Storage::fake('pages');
        $a = Account::factory()->superAdmin()->create();
        $this->actingAs($a)->withSession(['session_version' => $a->session_version]);
    }

    private function htmlFile(string $body = '<h1>Seating</h1><script>document.title="x"</script>'): UploadedFile
    {
        return UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('seating.html', '<!doctype html><html><body>'.$body.'</body></html>');
    }

    public function test_upload_list_serve_sandboxed_and_delete(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.pages.store'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'title' => 'Grade VII seating', 'file' => $this->htmlFile()])->assertRedirect(route('admin.pages.index'));
        $page = HtmlPage::firstOrFail();

        $this->get(route('admin.pages.index'))->assertOk()->assertSee('Grade VII seating')->assertSee('#'.$page->id);

        $res = $this->get(route('pages.sandboxed', $page->token));
        $res->assertOk()->assertSee('Seating');
        $csp = $res->headers->get('Content-Security-Policy');
        $this->assertStringContainsString('sandbox', $csp);
        $this->assertStringNotContainsString('allow-same-origin', $csp);
        $this->assertSame('nosniff', $res->headers->get('X-Content-Type-Options'));
        $this->assertStringContainsString('text/html', $res->headers->get('Content-Type'));

        $this->delete(route('admin.pages.destroy', $page), ['actor_name' => 'IT'])->assertRedirect();
        $this->get(route('pages.sandboxed', $page->token))->assertNotFound();
        $this->assertSame(['html_page.uploaded', 'html_page.deleted'], AuditEntry::orderBy('id')->pluck('action')->all());
    }

    public function test_rejects_non_html_and_large_files(): void
    {
        $this->post(route('admin.pages.store'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'title' => 'X', 'file' => UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('x.html', "%PDF-1.4 binary")])->assertSessionHasErrors('file');
        $this->post(route('admin.pages.store'), ['actor_name' => 'IT', 'title' => 'X', 'file' => UploadedFile::fake()->create('big.html', 3000, 'text/html')])->assertSessionHasErrors('file');
    }

    public function test_editor_cannot_upload(): void
    {
        $e = Account::factory()->editor()->create();
        $this->actingAs($e)->withSession(['session_version' => $e->session_version]);
        $this->post(route('admin.pages.store'), ['actor_name' => 'X', 'title' => 'X', 'file' => $this->htmlFile()])->assertForbidden();
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Content/SettingsService.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Content;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Models\CalendarType;
use App\Models\Grade;
use App\Models\NewsletterSeries;
use App\Models\Setting;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class SettingsService
{
    public function __construct(private readonly AuditLogger $audit) {}

    public function snapshot(): array
    {
        return [
            'homepage_count' => collect(Section::cases())->mapWithKeys(fn ($s) => [$s->value => (int) Setting::get('homepage_count.'.$s->value)])->all(),
            'academic_year_start_month' => (int) Setting::get('academic_year_start_month'),
            'grades' => Grade::ordered()->get(['id', 'label', 'position', 'hidden'])->toArray(),
            'calendar_types' => CalendarType::orderBy('id')->get(['id', 'name', 'hidden'])->toArray(),
            'newsletter_series' => NewsletterSeries::orderBy('id')->get(['id', 'name', 'hidden'])->toArray(),
        ];
    }

    public function update(array $input, Actor $actor): void
    {
        $valid = Validator::make($input, [
            'homepage_count' => ['required', 'array'],
            'homepage_count.info' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:1,20'],
            'homepage_count.circular' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:1,20'],
            'homepage_count.event' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:1,20'],
            'homepage_count.newsletter' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:1,20'],
            'academic_year_start_month' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:1,12'],
            'grades' => ['required', 'array', 'min:1'],
            'grades.*.id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:grades,id'],
            'grades.*.label' => ['required', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'grades.*.position' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:0'],
            'grades.*.hidden' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
            'calendar_types' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'calendar_types.*.id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:calendar_types,id'],
            'calendar_types.*.name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:40'],
            'calendar_types.*.hidden' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
            'newsletter_series' => ['nullable', 'array'],
            'newsletter_series.*.id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:newsletter_series,id'],
            'newsletter_series.*.name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:80'],
            'newsletter_series.*.hidden' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
        ])->validate();

        foreach (['grades' => 'label', 'calendar_types' => 'name', 'newsletter_series' => 'name'] as $list => $field) {
            $names = array_map(fn ($r) => mb_strtolower(trim($r[$field])), $valid[$list] ?? []);
            if (count($names) !== count(array_unique($names))) {
                throw ValidationException::withMessages([$list => 'Two entries have the same name. Each must be different.']);
            }
        }

        DB::transaction(function () use ($valid, $actor) {
            $before = $this->snapshot();

            foreach ($valid['homepage_count'] as $section => $count) {
                Setting::put('homepage_count.'.$section, (int) $count);
            }
            Setting::put('academic_year_start_month', (int) $valid['academic_year_start_month']);

            // Two-phase rename avoids unique-key collisions when labels are swapped.
            $this->saveRows(Grade::class, $valid['grades'], fn ($r) => ['label' => trim($r['label']), 'position' => (int) $r['position'], 'hidden' => (bool) ($r['hidden'] ?? false)], 'label');
            $this->saveRows(CalendarType::class, $valid['calendar_types'] ?? [], fn ($r) => ['name' => trim($r['name']), 'hidden' => (bool) ($r['hidden'] ?? false)], 'name');
            $this->saveRows(NewsletterSeries::class, $valid['newsletter_series'] ?? [], fn ($r) => ['name' => trim($r['name']), 'hidden' => (bool) ($r['hidden'] ?? false)], 'name');

            $this->audit->record('settings.updated', $actor, null, $before, $this->snapshot());
        });
    }

    /** @param class-string<\Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model> $model */
    private function saveRows(string $model, array $rows, callable $map, string $uniqueField): void
    {
        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            if (! empty($row['id'])) {
                $model::whereKey($row['id'])->update([$uniqueField => '__tmp_'.$row['id']]);
            }
        }
        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            if (! empty($row['id'])) {
                $model::whereKey($row['id'])->update($map($row));
            } else {
                $model::create($map($row));
            }
        }
    }
}
```
