`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SettingsController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Content\SettingsService;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;

class SettingsController extends Controller
{
    public function edit(SettingsService $settings)
    {
        return view('admin.settings.edit', ['s' => $settings->snapshot()]);
    }

    public function update(NameOnlyRequest $request, SettingsService $settings)
    {
        $input = $request->except(['_token', '_method', 'actor_name']);
        // Ignore the blank "Add new…" row (also covers browsers with JavaScript off).
        foreach (['grades' => 'label', 'calendar_types' => 'name', 'newsletter_series' => 'name'] as $list => $field) {
            $input[$list] = array_values(array_filter($input[$list] ?? [], fn ($row) => ! empty($row['id']) || trim((string) ($row[$field] ?? '')) !== ''));
        }
        $settings->update($input, $request->actor());

        return redirect()->route('admin.settings.edit')->with('status', 'Settings saved.');
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/HtmlPageController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Audit\AuditLogger;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\NameOnlyRequest;
use App\Models\HtmlPage;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class HtmlPageController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private readonly AuditLogger $audit) {}

    public function index()
    {
        return view('admin.pages.index', ['pages' => HtmlPage::orderByDesc('id')->get()]);
    }

    public function store(NameOnlyRequest $request)
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:150'],
            'file' => ['required', 'file', 'max:2048'],
        ]);

        $file = $request->file('file');
        $head = (string) file_get_contents($file->getRealPath(), false, null, 0, 2048);
        $mime = (new \finfo(FILEINFO_MIME_TYPE))->file($file->getRealPath());
        if (! in_array($mime, ['text/html', 'text/plain'], true) || ! preg_match('/<(!doctype html|html|body|div|table|h1|p)\b/i', $head)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['file' => 'This is not an HTML page. Upload the .html file.']);
        }

        $page = DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $file, $request) {
            $page = HtmlPage::create(['token' => Str::random(32), 'title' => $data['title'], 'bytes' => $file->getSize()]);
            Storage::disk('pages')->putFileAs('', $file, $page->storagePath());
            $this->audit->record('html_page.uploaded', $request->actor(), $page, null, ['title' => $page->title, 'bytes' => $page->bytes, 'sha256' => hash_file('sha256', $file->getRealPath())]);

            return $page;
        });

        return redirect()->route('admin.pages.index')->with('status', "Uploaded. Use custom page #{$page->id} in a post's Custom HTML page block.");
    }

    public function destroy(NameOnlyRequest $request, HtmlPage $page)
    {
        DB::transaction(function () use ($page, $request) {
            $page->delete();
            $this->audit->record('html_page.deleted', $request->actor(), $page, ['title' => $page->title], null);
        });

        return back()->with('status', 'Custom page removed. Posts that used it will no longer show it.');
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/SandboxedPageController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\HtmlPage;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class SandboxedPageController extends Controller
{
    public function __invoke(string $token)
    {
        $page = HtmlPage::where('token', $token)->first();
        abort_if($page === null || ! Storage::disk('pages')->exists($page->storagePath()), 404);

        return response(Storage::disk('pages')->get($page->storagePath()), 200, [
            'Content-Type' => 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
            // Opaque origin: scripts may run but cannot read site cookies, storage or the admin.
            'Content-Security-Policy' => "sandbox allow-scripts allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox; default-src 'self' data: https:; script-src 'unsafe-inline' https:; style-src 'unsafe-inline' https:; frame-ancestors 'self'",
            'X-Content-Type-Options' => 'nosniff',
            'Referrer-Policy' => 'no-referrer',
            'X-Robots-Tag' => 'noindex',
            'Cache-Control' => 'public, max-age=300',
        ]);
    }
}
```

`cms/app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SystemController.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Setting;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class SystemController extends Controller
{
    public function show()
    {
        $bytes = 0;
        foreach (['media', 'pages'] as $disk) {
            foreach (Storage::disk($disk)->allFiles() as $file) {
                $bytes += Storage::disk($disk)->size($file);
            }
        }

        return view('admin.system.show', [
            'php' => PHP_VERSION,
            'db' => DB::selectOne('select version() as v')->v,
            'imageDriver' => config('cms.image_driver'),
            'extensions' => collect(['gd', 'imagick', 'exif', 'fileinfo', 'intl', 'pdo_mysql', 'zip'])->mapWithKeys(fn ($e) => [$e => extension_loaded($e)]),
            'schedulerSource' => Setting::get('scheduler.last_source'),
            'schedulerLastRun' => Setting::get('scheduler.last_run_at'),
            'storageMb' => round($bytes / 1048576, 1),
        ]);
    }
}
```

```blade
                        <td><input name="{{ $key }}[{{ $n }}][{{ $field }}]" placeholder="Add new…" data-optional-new-row></td>
                        @if ($hasPosition)<td><input type="number" name="{{ $key }}[{{ $n }}][position]" value="{{ ($s[$key][$n - 1]['position'] ?? 0) + 10 }}" min="0"></td>@endif
                        <td><input type="hidden" name="{{ $key }}[{{ $n }}][hidden]" value="0"></td>
                    </tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </fieldset>
        @endforeach

        @include('admin.partials.actor-name')
        <button>Save settings</button>
    </form>
</div>
<script>
  /* Drop the empty "Add new" rows before submit so they are not validated as required. */
  document.querySelector('[data-screen="settings"] form').addEventListener('submit', function () {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-optional-new-row]'), function (input) {
      if (input.value.trim() === '') {
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(input.closest('tr').querySelectorAll('input'), function (i) { i.disabled = true; });
      }
    });
  });
</script>
@endsection
```

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use App\Content\Section;
use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\AuditEntry;
use App\Models\CalendarEntry;
use App\Models\Post;
use Database\Seeders\DemoSeeder;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Tests\TestCase;

class DemoSeederTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    public function test_seeds_every_section_and_state(): void
    {
        Storage::fake('media');
        $this->seed(DemoSeeder::class);

        $this->assertSame(2, Account::count());
        $this->assertSame(8, Post::withTrashed()->ofSection(Section::Info)->count());
        $this->assertSame(6, Post::ofSection(Section::Circular)->count());
        $this->assertSame(4, Post::ofSection(Section::Event)->count());
        $this->assertSame(3, Post::ofSection(Section::Newsletter)->count());
        $this->assertSame(6, CalendarEntry::count());

        $states = Post::withTrashed()->get()->map->adminStatus()->unique()->sort()->values()->all();
        $this->assertSame(['deleted', 'draft', 'hidden', 'live', 'scheduled'], $states);
        $this->assertTrue(Post::whereNotNull('pinned_until')->exists());
        $this->assertGreaterThan(20, AuditEntry::count());
        $this->assertNull(app(\App\Audit\AuditChain::class)->firstBrokenId());
    }

    public function test_refuses_in_production(): void
    {
        $this->app['env'] = 'production';
        $this->expectException(\RuntimeException::class);
        (new DemoSeeder())->setContainer($this->app)->__invoke();
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Seeder**

`cms/database/seeders/DemoSeeder.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use App\Content\CalendarService;
use App\Content\PostService;
use App\Content\PublishOptions;
use App\Content\Section;
use App\Media\FileUploader;
use App\Models\Account;
use App\Models\CalendarType;
use App\Models\Grade;
use App\Models\MediaAsset;
use App\Models\NewsletterSeries;
use App\Models\Post;
use App\Support\Actor;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;
use RuntimeException;

class DemoSeeder extends Seeder
{
    private PostService $posts;
    private FileUploader $uploader;

    public function run(PostService $posts, CalendarService $calendar, FileUploader $uploader): void
    {
        if (app()->environment('production')) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Demo data must never be loaded on the live website.');
        }

        $this->posts = $posts;
        $this->uploader = $uploader;

        Account::updateOrCreate(['username' => 'editor'], ['role' => Account::EDITOR, 'password' => 'demo-editor-pass']);
        Account::updateOrCreate(['username' => 'superadmin'], ['role' => Account::SUPER_ADMIN, 'password' => 'demo-super-pass']);

        $now = CarbonImmutable::now();
        $g = fn (string $label) => Grade::where('label', $label)->value('id');
        $priya = new Actor('Ms. Priya (demo)', null, 'editor', '127.0.0.1', 'Demo seeder', null);
        $khan = new Actor('Mr. Khan (demo)', null, 'editor', '127.0.0.1', 'Demo seeder', null);
        $text = fn (string $html) => ['type' => 'text', 'html' => $html];

        // Information corner
        $this->live(Section::Info, 'Welcome to DPS Millennium: admissions for 2027-28 open soon', [$text('<p>Registration details will be announced here first. Families can follow the campus build on the Campus page.</p>')], $priya, pin: $now->addDays(14));
        $this->live(Section::Info, 'World Mental Health Day photography activity', [$text('<p>A photography activity for students of <strong>Grades IX–XII</strong>.</p><ul><li>Choose one theme</li><li>Submit on A4</li></ul>'), ['type' => 'link', 'label' => 'Read the guidelines', 'url' => 'https://example.org/guidelines']], $khan, grades: ['mode' => 'range', 'from' => $g('IX'), 'to' => $g('XII')]);
        $this->live(Section::Info, 'Grade VII and VIII half yearly seating arrangement', [$text('<p>Find your room below.</p>'), ['type' => 'table', 'has_header' => true, 'paste' => "Class\tRoom\tRoll numbers\nVII A\t101\t1–32\nVII B\t102\t1–30\nVIII A\t201\t1–31"]], $priya, grades: ['mode' => 'specific', 'ids' => [$g('VII'), $g('VIII')]]);
        $this->live(Section::Info, 'Hackathon 2026: register today', [$text('<p>Teams of three. Registration closes Friday.</p>'), ['type' => 'youtube', 'url' => 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ', 'mode' => 'player']], $khan, removeOn: null);
        $hidden = $this->live(Section::Info, 'Summer camp registration (closed)', [$text('<p>Registration has closed.</p>')], $priya, removeOn: $now->addMinutes(1));
        Post::whereKey($hidden->id)->update(['remove_on' => $now->subDay()]);
        $this->posts->publish(new Post(['section' => Section::Info]), ['title' => 'Annual Day rehearsal schedule', 'blocks' => [$text('<p>Rehearsals start next week.</p>')]], new PublishOptions(publishAt: $now->addDays(2)->setTime(7, 0)), $priya, false);
        $this->posts->saveDraft(null, Section::Info, ['title' => 'Draft: parent orientation notes', 'blocks' => [$text('<p>To be completed.</p>')]], $khan, false);
        $deleted = $this->live(Section::Info, 'Posted by mistake', [$text('<p>Wrong notice.</p>')], $priya);
        $this->posts->delete($deleted, $priya);

        // Academic circulars
        foreach (['I', 'II', 'III', 'IV', 'V', 'VI'] as $i => $grade) {
            $this->live(Section::Circular, "Grade {$grade} Weekly Test I syllabus 2026-27", [
                $text('<p>Please find the syllabus attached.</p>'),
                ['type' => 'pdf', 'files' => [
                    ['media_id' => $this->pdf("Grade {$grade} Main Subjects.pdf"), 'label' => 'Main subjects'],
                    ['media_id' => $this->pdf("Grade {$grade} French 2L.pdf"), 'label' => 'French 2L'],
                ]],
            ], $i % 2 ? $khan : $priya, grades: ['mode' => 'specific', 'ids' => [$g($grade)]], at: $now->subDays(6 - $i));
        }

        // Events corner
        foreach (['Grade XI assembly on kindness', 'Teachers’ Day felicitation', 'Inter-house quiz final', 'Sports day heats'] as $i => $title) {
            $cover = $this->image("event-{$i}-cover.jpg", [30 + $i * 40, 90, 160]);
            $gallery = array_map(fn ($n) => ['media_id' => $this->image("event-{$i}-{$n}.jpg", [120, 40 + $n * 30, 90]), 'caption' => "Moment {$n}"], range(1, 4));
            $this->posts->publish(new Post(['section' => Section::Event]), [
                'title' => $title,
                'blocks' => [$text('<p>Students and staff came together for this event.</p>'), ['type' => 'gallery', 'items' => $gallery]],
                'cover_media_id' => $cover,
                'event_date_start' => $now->subDays(10 + $i)->format('Y-m-d'),
            ], new PublishOptions(), $khan, false);
        }

        // Newsletters
        $kg = NewsletterSeries::firstOrCreate(['name' => 'Kindergarten Newsletter']);
        $junior = NewsletterSeries::firstOrCreate(['name' => 'Junior School Newsletter']);
        foreach ([[$kg, 'Term I', true], [$kg, 'Term II', false], [$junior, 'Term I', true]] as $i => [$series, $term, $usePdf]) {
            $this->posts->publish(new Post(['section' => Section::Newsletter]), [
                'title' => "{$series->name}: {$term}",
                'blocks' => [],
                'cover_media_id' => $this->image("newsletter-{$i}.jpg", [200, 160, 40]),
                'newsletter_series_id' => $series->id,
                'newsletter_academic_year' => '2026-27',
                'newsletter_term' => $term,
                'newsletter_pdf_media_id' => $usePdf ? $this->pdf("newsletter-{$i}.pdf") : null,
                'newsletter_link' => $usePdf ? null : 'https://example.org/newsletter-flipbook',
            ], new PublishOptions(), $priya, false);
        }

        // Calendar
        $holiday = CalendarType::where('name', 'Holiday')->value('id');
        $exam = CalendarType::where('name', 'Exam')->value('id');
        $y = $now->year;
        foreach ([
            ['National Sport Day', $holiday, "{$y}-02-10", null],
            ['Half yearly examinations', $exam, $now->addDays(20)->format('Y-m-d'), $now->addDays(31)->format('Y-m-d')],
            ['Weekly Test I', $exam, $now->addDays(5)->format('Y-m-d'), null],
            ['Winter break', $holiday, "{$y}-12-24", ($y + 1).'-01-04'],
            ['Qatar National Day', $holiday, "{$y}-12-18", null],
            ['Final examinations', $exam, ($y + 1).'-03-01', ($y + 1).'-03-15'],
        ] as [$title, $type, $start, $end]) {
            $calendar->create(['title' => $title, 'calendar_type_id' => $type, 'start_date' => $start, 'end_date' => $end, 'grades' => ['mode' => 'all']], $khan);
        }
    }

    private function live(Section $section, string $title, array $blocks, Actor $actor, ?CarbonImmutable $pin = null, array $grades = ['mode' => 'none'], ?CarbonImmutable $removeOn = null, ?CarbonImmutable $at = null): Post
    {
        $post = $this->posts->publish(new Post(['section' => $section]), ['title' => $title, 'blocks' => $blocks, 'grades' => $grades], new PublishOptions(removeOn: $removeOn, pinnedUntil: $pin), $actor, false);
        if ($at !== null) {
            Post::whereKey($post->id)->update(['first_published_at' => $at, 'sort_at' => $at, 'publish_at' => $at]);
        }

        return $post->refresh();
    }

    private function image(string $name, array $rgb): int
    {
        $img = imagecreatetruecolor(1600, 1000);
        imagefill($img, 0, 0, imagecolorallocate($img, ...$rgb));
        imagestring($img, 5, 40, 40, 'DPS Millennium demo image: '.$name, imagecolorallocate($img, 255, 255, 255));
        $path = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'demo').'.jpg';
        imagejpeg($img, $path, 85);

        return $this->uploader->upload(new UploadedFile($path, $name, 'image/jpeg', null, true), 'image')->id;
    }

    private function pdf(string $name): int
    {
        $path = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'demo').'.pdf';
        file_put_contents($path, "%PDF-1.4\n1 0 obj<</Type/Catalog/Pages 2 0 R>>endobj\n2 0 obj<</Type/Pages/Kids[]/Count 0>>endobj\ntrailer<</Root 1 0 R>>\n%%EOF\n");

        return $this->uploader->upload(new UploadedFile($path, $name, 'application/pdf', null, true), 'pdf')->id;
    }
}
```

In `cms/database/seeders/DatabaseSeeder.php` keep `run()` empty (demo data is opt-in only) and add a comment line: `// Demo data: php artisan db:seed --class=DemoSeeder (never in production)`.

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: a self-contained folder the user uploads to Claude Design. Every `data-screen` value used in Blade views appears in `01-screens.md`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the sync test**

`cms/tests/Feature/HandoffPackTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace Tests\Feature;

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\File;
use Tests\TestCase;

class HandoffPackTest extends TestCase
{
    private function pack(string $file): string
    {
        return File::get(base_path('../docs/cms/handoff/'.$file));
    }

    public function test_every_admin_screen_is_documented(): void
    {
        $screens = collect(File::allFiles(resource_path('views/admin')))
            ->flatMap(fn ($f) => preg_match_all('/data-screen="([a-z-]+)"/', $f->getContents(), $m) ? $m[1] : [])
            ->unique()->sort()->values();

        $doc = $this->pack('01-screens.md');
        foreach ($screens as $screen) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString("`{$screen}`", $doc, "Screen {$screen} missing from 01-screens.md");
        }
    }

    public function test_limits_match_config(): void
    {
        $doc = $this->pack('02-fields-and-limits.md');
        foreach (['title' => 'Title max', 'summary' => 'Summary max', 'calendar_note' => 'Calendar note max', 'gallery_images' => 'Gallery max photos', 'table_rows' => 'Table max rows', 'table_cols' => 'Table max columns'] as $key => $label) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString($label.': '.config('cms.limits.'.$key), $doc, $label);
        }
        foreach (['image_bytes' => 'Photo max MB', 'pdf_bytes' => 'PDF max MB', 'video_bytes' => 'Video max MB'] as $key => $label) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString($label.': '.(config('cms.limits.'.$key) / 1048576), $doc, $label);
        }
    }

    public function test_sample_data_is_valid_json_with_all_sections(): void
    {
        $data = json_decode($this->pack('04-sample-data.json'), true, flags: JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR);
        $this->assertEqualsCanonicalizing(['info', 'circular', 'event', 'newsletter'], array_unique(array_column($data['posts'], 'section')));
        $this->assertNotEmpty($data['calendar']);
        foreach (['text', 'image', 'gallery', 'pdf', 'link', 'youtube', 'video', 'table', 'html_page'] as $type) {
            $this->assertStringContainsString('"type": "'.$type.'"', $this->pack('04-sample-data.json'), "sample data lacks a {$type} block");
        }
    }

    public function test_wireframes_have_no_styling_decisions(): void
    {
        $html = $this->pack('05-wireframes.html');
        $this->assertDoesNotMatchRegularExpression('/font-family\s*:\s*(?!system-ui|monospace)/i', $html);
        $this->assertDoesNotMatchRegularExpression('/#(?!000\b|fff\b|888\b|ccc\b|eee\b|000000|ffffff)[0-9a-f]{3,6}\b/i', $html);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: README**

`docs/cms/handoff/README.md`:

```markdown
# DPS Millennium CMS: Handoff pack for Claude Design

This folder describes **what exists and how it behaves**. It deliberately contains **no visual design**. Colours, typography, layout, motion and brand application are yours to decide, following DPS Millennium brand rules and the existing site prototypes (`homepage/`, `about/`, `campus/`, `fee/`, `sponsors/`).

## Who uses it
- **Editors:** teachers with WhatsApp-level computer skills, sharing one login. Design for zero training, big obvious actions and plain language.
- **Super admin:** school IT. Has everything editors have, plus the change log, version restore, settings and security.
- **Parents and students:** public visitors, mostly on phones, some on old browsers. Public pages are Phase 2, but the post content blocks below are what they will see.

## What to design
1. **Admin screens** (`01-screens.md`). Every screen already works unstyled at `/staff-cms` (run the demo, see `../../superpowers/plans/2026-09-14-cms-phase1.md` Task 15).
2. **Every state** in `03-states.md`. Empty, error and edge states are required, not optional.
3. **Content block rendering** for public post pages (the `data-block` types in `06-data-shapes.md`).
4. **A tutorial animation** for editors: "posting a circular in 60 seconds".

## Rules that must survive the redesign
- **Name field:** the "Your name" field appears on **every** save, publish, delete, restore and settings change. It is **always empty** when shown. Never pre-fill it, never suggest names.
- **Publish panel:** the publish controls (now/schedule, remove-on date, pin + unpin date, small fix) may become a dialog, but all fields must remain.
- **Plain language:** say "Remove from the website on", not "expiry".
- **Recycle bin:** editors see it but cannot restore. Restoring is an IT action.
- **Custom HTML pages:** IT only. Editors see an existing block but cannot change it.
- **Hooks:** keep the `data-screen`, `data-region`, `data-state`, `data-block` attributes; tests and scripts rely on them.
- **No build step:** the admin must work without a build step, so CSS and plain JS files only. Grey-box structure is in `05-wireframes.html`.

## Files
| File | Contents |
|---|---|
| `01-screens.md` | Screen inventory with purpose, content and actions |
| `02-fields-and-limits.md` | Every input with its limit and wording |
| `03-states.md` | Every state to design |
| `04-sample-data.json` | Realistic content to design with |
| `05-wireframes.html` | Grey-box structure (open in a browser) |
| `06-data-shapes.md` | Post, block and calendar data shapes |
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Screens inventory**

`docs/cms/handoff/01-screens.md`:

```markdown
# Screens

Base path: `/staff-cms` (configurable). E = Editor, S = Super admin.

| `data-screen` | URL | Who | Purpose | Must contain |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `login` (login form) | `/login` | E, S | Sign in | Username, password, error "Username or password is incorrect.", lockout message "Too many attempts. Try again in N minutes.", "Forgot your password? Contact school IT." |
| `dashboard` | `/` | E, S | Start here | Primary actions **New post** and **Calendar entry**; lists: Scheduled (title, section, go-live time + relative), Pinned (unpin date, "Unpin now" with name), Drafts, Recently published |
| `choose-section` | `/posts/new` | E, S | Pick what to post | Four large choices: Information corner, Events corner, Academic circulars, Newsletter |
| `post-editor` | `/posts/new/{section}`, `/posts/{id}` | E, S | Write and publish | Status line (Draft/Scheduled/Live/Removed, Posted date, Updated date, public link, S: Version history); Basics (title, summary); Grades (none/all/range/pick); Event details (event only); Cover photo (event, newsletter); Newsletter issue fields; Content blocks (add, reorder, remove, per-block editors); autosave status line; Publish panel; Unschedule (scheduled only) |
| `post-preview` | opens in new tab | E, S | See it as parents will | Phone/Desktop toggle; rendered post; problems list if content invalid |
| `posts-index` | `/posts` | E, S | Find any post | Filters (section, status, title search); table (title + pinned marker, section, status, date, actions Edit / Duplicate / Delete with name) |
| `calendar` | `/calendar` | E, S | Holidays and exams | Month navigation; month grid with entries by type; add/edit form (title, type, start, end, grades, note, name); delete with name |
| `recycle-bin` | `/recycle-bin` | E (view), S (restore) | Undo deletes | Deleted posts with deleted date and "removed permanently on" date; S: Restore with name |
| `audit-index` | `/change-log` | S | Who changed what | "Check the log has not been altered" action; filters (name, action, dates, post id); table (#, when, name typed, login, action, item, IP, details) |
| `audit-show` | `/change-log/{id}` | S | One change in detail | When, name typed, login, IP, device, session, item, hash; Before/After table of changed fields |
| `post-versions` | `/posts/{id}/versions` | S | Restore older content | Versions (number, time, by, title), expandable rendered content, Restore with name |
| `settings` | `/settings` | S | Configure lists | Homepage count per section; academic year start month; editable lists for Grades (name, order, hidden), Calendar types, Newsletters, each with "Add new" |
| `security` | `/security` | S | Passwords & sessions | Change shared Editor password (explains everyone is logged out); change own password; log out everyone; active sessions table |
| `custom-pages` | `/custom-pages` | S | Isolated HTML pages | Explanation; upload (title, file, name); table (ID, title opens page, size, date, remove with name) |
| `system` | `/system` | S | Health check | PHP, database, image library, extensions, scheduled tasks status, storage used, backups status |

Global: navigation (Dashboard, New post, All posts, Calendar, Recycle bin; S adds Change log, Settings, Security, Custom pages, System), "Signed in as Editor/Super admin", Log out, success message banner (`role="status"`), error summary (`data-region="form-errors"`).
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Fields and limits**

`docs/cms/handoff/02-fields-and-limits.md`:

```markdown
# Fields and limits

## Global limits
- Title max: 150
- Summary max: 300
- Calendar note max: 500
- Gallery max photos: 60
- Table max rows: 500
- Table max columns: 20
- Photo max MB: 15
- PDF max MB: 20
- Video max MB: 50
- Your name: required, 1–80 characters, always blank
- Custom HTML page file: 2 MB, `.html`

## Post: common
| Field | Type | Required | Help text to show |
|---|---|---|---|
| Title | text | yes | none |
| Short summary | textarea | no | "Shown in lists and WhatsApp previews" |
| Grades | choice: Not grade-specific / All grades / A range (From, To) / Pick grades (checkboxes) | no | Displayed publicly as "Grades I–V", "Grade III", "Grades I, III, X", "All grades" |
| Content blocks | list | yes except Newsletter | see below |

## Post: by section
| Section | Extra fields |
|---|---|
| Events corner | Date the event happened (required), End date (optional), Cover photo (required) |
| Newsletter | Newsletter series (required, from Settings), Academic year (required, e.g. 2026-27), Term (required, free text e.g. "Term I"), Cover photo (required), **either** PDF **or** Link (exactly one) |
| Information corner, Academic circulars | none |

## Publish panel
| Field | Rules | Wording |
|---|---|---|
| Publish now / Schedule | schedule needs a future date+time, Qatar time | "Schedule for later (Qatar time)" |
| Remove from the website on | optional; must be after go-live | "Remove from the website on (optional)" |
| Pin | optional; requires Unpin on (future) | "Pin to the top of its box" / "Unpin on (required when pinned)" |
| Small fix | only when editing a live post | "Small fix, don't move it to the top". Note: "Parents will see “Updated” with today’s date either way." |
| Buttons | Draft: Save draft · Preview · Publish. Scheduled: Preview · Update schedule. Live: Preview · Save changes | none |

## Blocks
| Block | Editor inputs | Limits |
|---|---|---|
| Text | Bold, Italic, • List, 1. List, Link, Remove link | paste is converted to plain text |
| Photo | upload, caption, crop (x, y, w, h), max width | 15 MB; JPG/PNG/WebP (HEIC only if server supports) |
| Photo gallery | multi-upload, caption per photo, remove photo | 60 photos |
| PDF file(s) | multi-upload, label per PDF, remove | 20 PDFs per block, 20 MB each |
| Link button | label, web address | label 100; https only |
| YouTube video | link, "Show as a link" / "Show the video player" | YouTube URLs only |
| Short video | MP4 upload, caption | 50 MB |
| Table | "First row is a heading", paste box, grid of cells, + Row, + Column, − Last row, − Last column | 500 × 20, cell 500 chars |
| Custom HTML page | page ID, Link / Embed | Super admin only |

## Calendar entry
Title (150, required) · Type (Holiday/Exam, editable list) · Start date (required) · End date (optional, ≥ start) · Grades (optional) · Note (500) · Your name.

## Error messages (use verbatim or improve tone, keep meaning)
- "Type your name before saving. This is recorded in the change log."
- "This video is 80 MB. The limit is 50 MB. Upload it to YouTube and add a YouTube block instead."
- "\"IMG_2231.HEIC\" is an iPhone HEIC photo. Please send it as JPG … and upload again."
- "The table has 612 rows. The limit is 500. Split it into two tables or upload a PDF."
- "Pinned posts need an \"unpin on\" date."
- "The scheduled time has already passed. Choose a future time or publish now."
- "Only IT (Super admin) can add or change custom page blocks."
- "Add either a PDF or a link, not both."
```

- [ ] **Step 6: States**

`docs/cms/handoff/03-states.md`:

```markdown
# States to design

## Post status (`data-state` on post editor, list rows)
- `new`: not saved yet
- `draft`: saved, not public
- `scheduled`: will go live at a set time (show countdown)
- `live`: public
- `hidden`: past its "remove from website" date (public cannot see it)
- `deleted`: in recycle bin (show "removed permanently on …")
- pinned (combined with live): show unpin date

## Per screen
| Screen | States |
|---|---|
| Dashboard | each list empty ("Nothing scheduled.", "Nothing pinned.", "No drafts.", "Nothing published yet. Start with “New post”."); many items (20+ drafts) |
| Post editor | no blocks yet ("No content yet. Add a block below."); 30+ blocks; very long title (150 chars, no spaces); validation errors at top + next to fields; per-block error ("Block 3: The link must be a full web address…"); upload in progress; upload failed (message stays 12 s); restore-unsaved-changes bar; live post (Save changes + Small fix); scheduled post (Update schedule + Unschedule); newsletter with no series configured ("Ask IT to add one in Settings") |
| Blocks | text empty; photo not uploaded; photo uploaded with crop panel open; gallery with 1 and 60 photos; PDF with long label; table 1×1 and 500×20 (horizontal scroll); multi-line table cells; YouTube link mode vs player mode; custom page block as seen by Editor (locked) vs Super admin |
| Preview | valid; invalid content (problems list); phone width |
| All posts | no results for filters; 25+ pages |
| Calendar | month with no entries; day with 3+ entries; range crossing months; edit mode; validation error (end before start) |
| Recycle bin | empty; editor view (no restore); super admin view |
| Change log | empty filters result; integrity OK banner; integrity ALTERED warning banner |
| Change detail | no field changes (login events); large JSON values (blocks) |
| Settings | new row; duplicate name error |
| Security | password too short; confirmation mismatch; wrong current password; no sessions |
| Custom pages | none uploaded; wrong file type error |
| System | no cron (visitor mode note); missing PHP extension shown as "NO" |
| Login | wrong password; locked out (N minutes); session expired → back at login |
```

```html
<!doctype html>

<title>DPS Millennium CMS: grey-box wireframes (structure only)</title>
<style>
  /* Structure only. No brand colours or fonts: Claude Design decides those. */
  body { font-family: system-ui; margin: 0; padding: 16px; background: #fff; color: #000; }
  h1 { font-size: 20px; } h2 { font-size: 16px; margin: 32px 0 8px; }
  .frame { border: 2px solid #000; max-width: 1100px; margin-bottom: 40px; }
  .phone { max-width: 390px; }
  .bar { border-bottom: 1px solid #888; padding: 8px; }
  .row { display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; padding: 8px; }
  .col { flex: 1 1 280px; border: 1px dashed #888; padding: 8px; min-height: 60px; }
  .box { border: 1px solid #888; padding: 8px; margin: 8px; }
  .btn { display: inline-block; border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px 10px; margin: 2px; }
  .big { padding: 18px 24px; }
  .field { border: 1px solid #888; height: 28px; margin: 4px 0 10px; }
  .area { border: 1px solid #888; height: 80px; margin: 4px 0 10px; }
  .muted { color: #888; font-size: 13px; }
  .grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr); gap: 2px; padding: 8px; }
  .grid div { border: 1px solid #ccc; min-height: 48px; font-size: 12px; padding: 2px; }
  .notice { border: 1px solid #000; padding: 8px; margin: 8px; }
</style>
</head>
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Data shapes**

`docs/cms/handoff/06-data-shapes.md`:

````markdown
# Data shapes

## Post
```json
{
  "id": 21,
  "section": "info | event | circular | newsletter",
  "title": "string ≤150",
  "summary": "string ≤300 | null",
  "slug": "string (fixed at first publish)",
  "url": "/{information|events|circulars|newsletters}/{id}-{slug}",
  "grades": { "mode": "none | all | range | specific", "ids": [4, 5, 6], "label": "Grades I–III | null" },
  "status": "draft | scheduled | live | hidden | deleted",
  "posted_at": "ISO-8601 +03:00 (first publish)",
  "updated_at": "ISO-8601 +03:00 | null (shown as Updated)",
  "pinned_until": "ISO-8601 | null",
  "remove_on": "ISO-8601 | null",
  "academic_year": "2026-27",
  "event": { "date_start": "YYYY-MM-DD", "date_end": "YYYY-MM-DD | null" },
  "cover": { "media_id": 60 },
  "newsletter": { "series": "Kindergarten Newsletter", "term": "Term I", "pdf_media_id": 71, "link": null },
  "blocks": ["see below"]
}
```

## Blocks (`data-block` = `type`)
| type | fields |
|---|---|
| `text` | `html` (only p, br, strong/b, em/i, ul, ol, li, a[href]) |
| `image` | `media_id`, `caption` |
| `gallery` | `items[]: {media_id, caption}` |
| `pdf` | `files[]: {media_id, label}` |
| `link` | `label`, `url` |
| `youtube` | `url`, `video_id`, `mode: link | player` |
| `video` | `media_id`, `caption` |
| `table` | `has_header`, `rows: string[][]` (cells may contain line breaks) |
| `html_page` | `html_page_id`, `mode: link | embed` (embed is a sandboxed iframe) |

All blocks also carry `"version": 1`.

## Media URLs
`/media/{token}/{rev}/{file}` where `file` ∈:
- Images: `400.webp`, `400.jpg`, `800.webp`, `800.jpg`, `1600.webp`, `1600.jpg`, `master.jpg`. Serve WebP with JPG fallback via `<picture>`.
- PDF: `file.pdf`; add `?download=1` to force download.
- Video: `file.mp4`.

## Calendar entry
```json
{ "title": "Half yearly examinations", "type": "Exam", "start_date": "2026-10-04", "end_date": "2026-10-15", "grades": { "label": "All grades" }, "note": null }
```

## Homepage boxes
Newest *N* public posts per section, pinned first. *N* is a setting (defaults: Info 5, Circulars 4, Events 8, Newsletter 4). The public JSON API arrives in Phase 2 with these shapes.
````

- [ ] **Step 10: Run tests**

Run: `php artisan test`
Expected: all pass. If the screen test fails, the listed `data-screen` value is missing from `01-screens.md`. Add a row; do not rename hooks. The login view has no `data-screen`: add `data-screen="login"` to the `<h1>`'s parent in `admin/auth/login.blade.php` by wrapping the form in `<div data-screen="login">…</div>`.

- [ ] **Step 11: Commit**

```bash
git add docs/cms/handoff cms/tests/Feature/HandoffPackTest.php cms/resources/views/admin/auth/login.blade.php
```

## Out of Phase 1 (next plans)

- **Phase 2:** public post pages, archive + FULLTEXT search, public calendar, homepage data injection, JSON API, caching, sitemap, and integration of Claude Design's designs.
- **Phase 3:** security headers/CSP/HSTS, production error pages, restricted DB grants on `audit_entries`, backups, staging deploy, hosting requirements sheet, IT handover guide.
- **Later:** media gallery (flagged important), leadership profiles, Apply Now, parent/student login backend, payments, PDF text search.
